========================================================================================
// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical/README.md
========================================================================================

# Dynamic Topography Adjoint Benchmark: 2D Spherical Shell

This benchmark computes cellwise density and viscosity kernels for the
dynamic-topography objective in an instantaneous spherical-shell model.
Selected physical-property cell perturbations are validated with centered
finite differences.

Run the benchmark from a build directory with:

```bash
./aspect ../benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical/adjoint_dynamic_topography.prm
```

Then create the cellwise density/viscosity kernel plot:

```bash
../benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical/scripts/plot_cellwise_kernels.py \
  output-adjoint-dynamic-topography-2d-spherical \
  --output ../benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical/doc/cellwise_density_viscosity_kernels.png
```

To run the finite-difference validation, first build the benchmark material
wrapper:

```bash
cmake -S ../benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical \
  -B adjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical_plugin \
  -D Aspect_DIR=$(pwd)
cmake --build adjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical_plugin
cp adjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical_plugin/libadjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical.release.so \
  ../benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical/
```

Then run:

```bash
../benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical/scripts/run_fd_validation.py \
  --aspect ./aspect \
  --selection largest \
  --cells-per-property 32
```

The validation wrapper perturbs physical density or viscosity directly in a
selected active cell after material evaluation. Therefore the finite-difference
directional derivative is compared with the corresponding physical-property
adjoint kernel times cell volume.

========================================================================================
// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical/README.md

========================================================================================
// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical/adjoint_dynamic_topography.prm
========================================================================================

# Benchmark for the instantaneous dynamic-topography adjoint.
#
# This parameter file computes cellwise adjoint kernels for density and
# viscosity controls in a spherical shell model with temperature/composition-
# dependent material properties. The initial temperature is 1600 K with a
# 1700 K circular anomaly centered at (0, 6.1e6 m) with radius 2.5e5 m. The
# single compositional field is 0 below radius 6.0e6 m and 1 above it.

set Dimension                              = 2
set CFL number                             = 1.0
set End time                               = 0
set Start time                             = 0
set Output directory                       = output-adjoint-dynamic-topography-2d-spherical
set Adiabatic surface temperature          = 1600
set Pressure normalization                 = surface
set Surface pressure                       = 0
set Use years instead of seconds           = false
set Nonlinear solver scheme                = no Advection, adjoint Stokes

subsection Adjoint
  set List of objectives = dynamic topography

  subsection Dynamic topography
    set Target topography model = function

    subsection Function
      set Coordinate system = spherical
      set Variable names = r,phi
      set Function expression = 1000 * sin(phi)
    end
  end
end

subsection Boundary temperature model
  set List of model names = spherical constant
  set Fixed temperature boundary indicators   = inner, outer

  subsection Spherical constant
    set Inner temperature = 1600
    set Outer temperature = 1600
  end
end

subsection Gravity model
  set Model name = radial constant

  subsection Radial constant
    set Magnitude = 10
  end
end

subsection Geometry model
  set Model name = spherical shell

  subsection Spherical shell
    set Inner radius = 3480000
    set Outer radius = 6336000
  end
end

subsection Initial temperature model
  set Model name = function

  subsection Function
    set Variable names     = x,y
    set Function expression = if(sqrt(x*x + (y-6100000)*(y-6100000)) < 250000, 1700, 1600)
  end
end

subsection Compositional fields
  set Number of fields = 1
end

subsection Initial composition model
  set Model name = function

  subsection Function
    set Variable names      = x,y
    set Function expression = if(sqrt(x*x + y*y) < 6000000, 0.0, 1.0)
  end
end

subsection Material model
  set Model name = simple

  subsection Simple model
    set Reference density             = 3300
    set Reference specific heat       = 1250
    set Reference temperature         = 1600
    set Thermal conductivity          = 0
    set Thermal expansion coefficient = 3e-4
    set Viscosity                     = 1e21
    set Composition viscosity prefactor = 1
    set Thermal viscosity exponent    = 36.84136
  end
end

subsection Mesh refinement
  set Initial adaptive refinement        = 0
  set Initial global refinement          = 2
end

subsection Boundary velocity model
  set Tangential velocity boundary indicators = inner, outer
end

subsection Postprocess
  set List of postprocessors = dynamic topography, adjoint kernels
end

// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical/adjoint_dynamic_topography.prm

// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical/cellwise_perturbation.cc

/*
  Copyright (C) 2011 - 2024 by the authors of the ASPECT code.

  This file is part of ASPECT.

  ASPECT is free software; you can redistribute it and/or modify
  it under the terms of the GNU General Public License as published by
  the Free Software Foundation; either version 2, or (at your option)
  any later version.
*/

#include <aspect/material_model/interface.h>
#include <aspect/material_model/simple.h>
#include <aspect/simulator_access.h>

#include <cstdlib>

namespace aspect
{
  namespace MaterialModel
  {
    template <int dim>
    class AdjointCellwisePerturbation : public Simple<dim>
    {
      public:
        void
        evaluate(const MaterialModel::MaterialModelInputs<dim> &in,
                 MaterialModel::MaterialModelOutputs<dim> &out) const override;

        void
        parse_parameters(ParameterHandler &prm) override;

      private:
        int perturbed_active_cell_index;
        std::string perturbed_property;
        double perturbation_amplitude;
    };



    template <int dim>
    void
    AdjointCellwisePerturbation<dim>::evaluate(const MaterialModel::MaterialModelInputs<dim> &in,
                                               MaterialModel::MaterialModelOutputs<dim> &out) const
    {
      Simple<dim>::evaluate(in, out);

      if (perturbed_active_cell_index < 0
          || perturbation_amplitude == 0.0
          || in.current_cell.state() != IteratorState::valid
          || static_cast<int>(in.current_cell->active_cell_index()) != perturbed_active_cell_index)
        return;

      if (perturbed_property == "density")
        for (double &density : out.densities)
          density += perturbation_amplitude;
      else if (perturbed_property == "viscosity")
        for (double &viscosity : out.viscosities)
          viscosity += perturbation_amplitude;
    }


    template <int dim>
    void
    AdjointCellwisePerturbation<dim>::parse_parameters(ParameterHandler &prm)
    {
      Simple<dim>::parse_parameters(prm);

      const char *cell = std::getenv("ASPECT_ADJOINT_FD_CELL");
      const char *property = std::getenv("ASPECT_ADJOINT_FD_PROPERTY");
      const char *amplitude = std::getenv("ASPECT_ADJOINT_FD_AMPLITUDE");

      perturbed_active_cell_index = (cell != nullptr ? std::atoi(cell) : -1);
      perturbed_property = (property != nullptr ? property : "none");
      perturbation_amplitude = (amplitude != nullptr ? std::atof(amplitude) : 0.0);
    }
  }
}

namespace aspect
{
  namespace MaterialModel
  {
    ASPECT_REGISTER_MATERIAL_MODEL(AdjointCellwisePerturbation,
                                   "adjoint cellwise perturbation",
                                   "A benchmark material model that extends the simple model "
                                   "with an optional additive density or viscosity perturbation "
                                   "on one active cell. It is used only by the dynamic-topography "
                                   "adjoint finite-difference validation script.")
  }
}

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Plot cellwise dynamic-topography adjoint density and viscosity kernels."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import os
from pathlib import Path

os.environ.setdefault("MPLCONFIGDIR", str(Path(".matplotlib-cache").resolve()))
os.environ.setdefault("XDG_CACHE_HOME", str(Path(".cache").resolve()))

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import pandas as pd


def read_adjoint_table(path: Path, columns: list[str]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    with path.open() as stream:
        for line in stream:
            if not line.strip() or line.startswith("#"):
                continue
            rows.append(line.rstrip("\n").split("\t"))

    data = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=columns)
    for column in columns:
        if column not in {"objective", "term", "property", "control"}:
            data[column] = pd.to_numeric(data[column])
    return data


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument(
        "output_directory",
        type=Path,
        help="ASPECT output directory containing adjoint_kernels_rank_00000.txt",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output",
        type=Path,
        default=Path("doc/cellwise_density_viscosity_kernels.png"),
        help="Figure path to write.",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    kernel_file = args.output_directory / "adjoint_kernels_rank_00000.txt"
    kernels = read_adjoint_table(
        kernel_file,
        ["objective", "term", "property", "active_cell_index", "cell_volume", "value"],
    )

    terms = kernels[kernels["property"].isin(["density", "viscosity"])].copy()
    grouped = (
        terms.groupby(["property", "active_cell_index"], as_index=False)["value"]
        .sum()
        .sort_values(["property", "active_cell_index"])
    )

    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(9, 3.5), constrained_layout=True)
    for axis, property_name in zip(axes, ["density", "viscosity"]):
        subset = grouped[grouped["property"] == property_name]
        axis.axhline(0.0, color="0.3", linewidth=0.8)
        axis.plot(subset["active_cell_index"], subset["value"], marker="o", linewidth=1.2)
        axis.set_title(property_name)
        axis.set_xlabel("active cell index")
        axis.set_ylabel("cell-average kernel")

    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fig.savefig(args.output, dpi=200)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

========================================================================================
// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical/scripts/plot_cellwise_kernels.py

// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical/scripts/run_fd_validation.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Run a cellwise finite-difference validation for the adjoint benchmark."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import math
import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

os.environ.setdefault("MPLCONFIGDIR", str(Path(".matplotlib-cache").resolve()))
os.environ.setdefault("XDG_CACHE_HOME", str(Path(".cache").resolve()))

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt


SCRIPT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
BENCHMARK_DIR = SCRIPT_DIR.parent


def replace_parameter(text: str, parameter: str, value: str) -> str:
    pattern = re.compile(rf"^(\s*set\s+{re.escape(parameter)}\s*=\s*).*$", re.MULTILINE)
    updated, count = pattern.subn(rf"\g<1>{value}", text, count=1)
    if count != 1:
        raise ValueError(f"Could not find unique parameter line for '{parameter}'.")
    return updated


def insert_plugin_material_model(text: str, plugin: Path) -> str:
    if "set Additional shared libraries" not in text:
        text = f"set Additional shared libraries            = {plugin}\n" + text
    else:
        text = replace_parameter(text, "Additional shared libraries", str(plugin))

    material_header = "subsection Material model\n  set Model name = simple"
    if material_header not in text:
        raise ValueError("Could not find the simple material model block.")
    text = text.replace(material_header,
                        "subsection Material model\n  set Model name = adjoint cellwise perturbation",
                        1)

    return text


def write_case_prm(
    base_text: str,
    prm_path: Path,
    output_directory: Path,
) -> None:
    text = replace_parameter(base_text, "Output directory", str(output_directory))
    prm_path.write_text(text)


def run_aspect(aspect: Path, prm_path: Path, log_path: Path, environment: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> None:
    aspect_executable = aspect.resolve() if aspect.exists() else aspect
    env = os.environ.copy()
    if environment is not None:
        env.update(environment)
    with log_path.open("w") as log:
        result = subprocess.run(
            [str(aspect_executable), str(prm_path)],
            stdout=log,
            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            text=True,
            env=env,
        )

    if result.returncode != 0:
        tail = "\n".join(log_path.read_text(errors="replace").splitlines()[-40:])
        raise RuntimeError(f"ASPECT failed for {prm_path}:\n{tail}")


def read_objective(output_directory: Path, objective_name: str) -> float:
    kernel_file = output_directory / "adjoint_kernels_rank_00000.txt"
    with kernel_file.open() as stream:
        for line in stream:
            fields = line.rstrip("\n").split("\t")
            if len(fields) == 3 and fields[0] == "# objective_value" and fields[1] == objective_name:
                return float(fields[2])
    raise ValueError(f"Could not find objective '{objective_name}' in {kernel_file}.")


def read_property_kernels(output_directory: Path) -> list[dict[str, float | str]]:
    kernel_file = output_directory / "adjoint_kernels_rank_00000.txt"
    contributions: dict[tuple[str, str, int], dict[str, float | str]] = {}
    with kernel_file.open() as stream:
        for line in stream:
            if not line.strip() or line.startswith("#"):
                continue
            objective, _term, property_name, cell_index, cell_volume, value = line.rstrip("\n").split("\t")
            if property_name not in {"density", "viscosity"}:
                continue

            key = (objective, property_name, int(cell_index))
            if key not in contributions:
                contributions[key] = {
                    "objective": objective,
                    "property": property_name,
                    "active_cell_index": int(cell_index),
                    "cell_volume": float(cell_volume),
                    "value": 0.0,
                }
            contributions[key]["value"] = float(contributions[key]["value"]) + float(value)

    return list(contributions.values())


def selected_kernel_rows(
    rows: list[dict[str, float | str]],
    objective_name: str,
    cells_per_property: int,
    selection: str,
    explicit_cells: list[int] | None,
) -> list[dict[str, float | str]]:
    selected: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
    for property_name in ["density", "viscosity"]:
        subset = [
            row for row in rows
            if row["objective"] == objective_name and row["property"] == property_name
        ]
        if explicit_cells is not None:
            selected.extend(row for row in subset if int(row["active_cell_index"]) in explicit_cells)
        else:
            if selection == "largest":
                selected.extend(
                    sorted(
                        subset,
                        key=lambda row: abs(float(row["value"]) * float(row["cell_volume"])),
                        reverse=True,
                    )[:cells_per_property]
                )
            else:
                sorted_subset = sorted(
                    subset,
                    key=lambda row: float(row["value"]) * float(row["cell_volume"]),
                )
                if cells_per_property >= len(sorted_subset):
                    selected.extend(sorted_subset)
                else:
                    selected_indices = {
                        round(i * (len(sorted_subset) - 1) / (cells_per_property - 1))
                        for i in range(cells_per_property)
                    }
                    selected.extend(sorted_subset[i] for i in sorted(selected_indices))
    return selected


def l2_relative_error(fd_values: list[float], adjoint_values: list[float]) -> float:
    numerator = sum((adjoint - fd) * (adjoint - fd)
                    for fd, adjoint in zip(fd_values, adjoint_values))
    denominator = sum(fd * fd for fd in fd_values)
    return math.sqrt(numerator / denominator) if denominator > 0.0 else 0.0


def pearson_correlation(fd_values: list[float], adjoint_values: list[float]) -> float:
    if len(fd_values) < 2:
        return 1.0

    mean_fd = sum(fd_values) / len(fd_values)
    mean_adjoint = sum(adjoint_values) / len(adjoint_values)
    covariance = sum((fd - mean_fd) * (adjoint - mean_adjoint)
                     for fd, adjoint in zip(fd_values, adjoint_values))
    variance_fd = sum((fd - mean_fd) * (fd - mean_fd) for fd in fd_values)
    variance_adjoint = sum((adjoint - mean_adjoint) * (adjoint - mean_adjoint)
                           for adjoint in adjoint_values)
    denominator = math.sqrt(variance_fd * variance_adjoint)
    return covariance / denominator if denominator > 0.0 else 1.0


def plot_results(rows: list[dict[str, float | str]], output: Path) -> None:
    plt.rcParams.update(
        {
            "font.size": 9,
            "axes.titlesize": 12,
            "axes.labelsize": 10,
            "xtick.labelsize": 8.5,
            "ytick.labelsize": 8.5,
            "legend.fontsize": 8.5,
            "axes.linewidth": 0.8,
        }
    )

    colors = {
        "density": "#2a9d8f",
        "viscosity": "#e76f51",
    }

    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(8.8, 4.0), constrained_layout=True)
    fig.patch.set_facecolor("white")

    for axis, control in zip(axes, ["density", "viscosity"]):
        subset = [row for row in rows if row["control"] == control]
        fd_values = [float(row["finite_difference"]) for row in subset]
        adjoint_values = [float(row["adjoint"]) for row in subset]
        relative_errors = [float(row["relative_error"]) for row in subset]

        axis.set_facecolor("#fbfbfa")
        axis.grid(True, which="major", color="#e7e3dd", linewidth=0.7)
        axis.axhline(0.0, color="#c8c4bd", linewidth=0.8, zorder=0)
        axis.axvline(0.0, color="#c8c4bd", linewidth=0.8, zorder=0)
        axis.scatter(fd_values,
                     adjoint_values,
                     s=42,
                     color=colors[control],
                     alpha=0.9,
                     edgecolor="white",
                     linewidth=0.7,
                     zorder=3)
        if fd_values and adjoint_values:
            raw_lower = min(fd_values + adjoint_values)
            raw_upper = max(fd_values + adjoint_values)
            if raw_lower >= 0.0:
                lower = 0.0
                upper = raw_upper * 1.08
            elif raw_upper <= 0.0:
                lower = raw_lower * 1.08
                upper = 0.0
            else:
                bound = max(abs(raw_lower), abs(raw_upper))
                lower = -1.08 * bound
                upper = 1.08 * bound
            axis.plot([lower, upper],
                      [lower, upper],
                      color="#202020",
                      linewidth=1.15,
                      label="1:1",
                      zorder=2)
            axis.set_xlim(lower, upper)
            axis.set_ylim(lower, upper)
            axis.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="box")

        annotation = (
            f"n = {len(subset)}\n"
            f"max rel. err. = {max(relative_errors):.2e}\n"
            f"L2 rel. err. = {l2_relative_error(fd_values, adjoint_values):.2e}\n"
            f"r = {pearson_correlation(fd_values, adjoint_values):.6f}"
        )
        axis.text(0.04,
                  0.96,
                  annotation,
                  transform=axis.transAxes,
                  va="top",
                  ha="left",
                  fontsize=8,
                  color="#333333",
                  bbox={
                      "boxstyle": "round,pad=0.32",
                      "facecolor": "white",
                      "edgecolor": "#ded8cf",
                      "linewidth": 0.7,
                      "alpha": 0.92,
                  })
        axis.set_title(control.capitalize(), pad=8)
        axis.set_xlabel("Finite difference")
        axis.set_ylabel("Adjoint kernel prediction")
        axis.ticklabel_format(axis="both", style="sci", scilimits=(-2, 3), useOffset=False)
        axis.legend(frameon=False, loc="lower right", handlelength=2.4)
        for spine in axis.spines.values():
            spine.set_color("#222222")

    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fig.savefig(output, dpi=220, facecolor=fig.get_facecolor())


def parse_cell_list(value: str | None) -> list[int] | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    return [int(item.strip()) for item in value.split(",") if item.strip()]


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--aspect", type=Path, default=Path("./aspect"), help="Path to the ASPECT executable.")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--plugin",
        type=Path,
        default=BENCHMARK_DIR / "libadjoint_dynamic_topography_2d_spherical.so",
        help="Path to the benchmark material plugin.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--prm",
        type=Path,
        default=BENCHMARK_DIR / "adjoint_dynamic_topography.prm",
        help="Base benchmark prm file.",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--workdir", type=Path, default=Path("fd_validation_work"))
    parser.add_argument("--density-step", type=float, default=1.0)
    parser.add_argument("--viscosity-step", type=float, default=1.0e18)
    parser.add_argument("--objective", default="dynamic topography")
    parser.add_argument("--cells-per-property", type=int, default=32)
    parser.add_argument("--selection", choices=["quantile", "largest"], default="largest")
    parser.add_argument("--cells", help="Comma-separated active cell indices. Defaults to the largest adjoint kernels.")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--csv",
        type=Path,
        default=BENCHMARK_DIR / "doc" / "fd_vs_adjoint_validation.csv",
        help="Validation table to write.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--figure",
        type=Path,
        default=BENCHMARK_DIR / "doc" / "fd_vs_adjoint_validation.png",
        help="Validation figure to write.",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    plugin = args.plugin.resolve()
    variant_plugin = plugin.with_name(plugin.name.replace(".so", ".release.so"))
    if not plugin.exists() and not variant_plugin.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Benchmark plugin not found: {plugin} or {variant_plugin}")

    base_text = insert_plugin_material_model(args.prm.read_text(), plugin)
    args.workdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    baseline_dir = args.workdir / "baseline"
    if baseline_dir.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(baseline_dir)
    baseline_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    write_case_prm(base_text, baseline_dir / "baseline.prm", baseline_dir / "output")
    run_aspect(args.aspect, baseline_dir / "baseline.prm", baseline_dir / "aspect.log")

    kernels = read_property_kernels(baseline_dir / "output")
    selected_rows = selected_kernel_rows(
        kernels,
        args.objective,
        args.cells_per_property,
        args.selection,
        parse_cell_list(args.cells),
    )

    rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
    for kernel in selected_rows:
        property_name = str(kernel["property"])
        cell_index = int(kernel["active_cell_index"])
        step = args.density_step if property_name == "density" else args.viscosity_step
        objectives: dict[str, float] = {}

        for sign_name, sign in [("minus", -1.0), ("plus", 1.0)]:
            case_name = f"{property_name}_{cell_index}_{sign_name}"
            case_dir = args.workdir / case_name
            if case_dir.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(case_dir)
            case_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
            write_case_prm(
                base_text,
                case_dir / f"{case_name}.prm",
                case_dir / "output",
            )
            run_aspect(
                args.aspect,
                case_dir / f"{case_name}.prm",
                case_dir / "aspect.log",
                {
                    "ASPECT_ADJOINT_FD_CELL": str(cell_index),
                    "ASPECT_ADJOINT_FD_PROPERTY": property_name,
                    "ASPECT_ADJOINT_FD_AMPLITUDE": f"{sign * step:.16g}",
                },
            )
            objectives[sign_name] = read_objective(case_dir / "output", args.objective)

        finite_difference = (objectives["plus"] - objectives["minus"]) / (2.0 * step)
        adjoint = float(kernel["value"]) * float(kernel["cell_volume"])
        scale = max(abs(finite_difference), abs(adjoint), 1.0e-300)
        rows.append(
            {
                "control": property_name,
                "active_cell_index": cell_index,
                "cell_volume": float(kernel["cell_volume"]),
                "step": step,
                "objective_minus": objectives["minus"],
                "objective_plus": objectives["plus"],
                "finite_difference": finite_difference,
                "adjoint": adjoint,
                "absolute_error": abs(finite_difference - adjoint),
                "relative_error": abs(finite_difference - adjoint) / scale,
            }
        )

    args.csv.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with args.csv.open("w", newline="") as stream:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(stream, fieldnames=list(rows[0].keys()))
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)

    plot_results(rows, args.figure)

    for row in rows:
        print(
            f"{row['control']} cell {row['active_cell_index']}: "
            f"FD={row['finite_difference']:.8e}, adjoint={row['adjoint']:.8e}, "
            f"relative_error={row['relative_error']:.3e}"
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

========================================================================================
// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical/README.md
========================================================================================

# Dynamic Topography Adjoint Benchmark: 3D Spherical Shell

This benchmark computes cellwise density and viscosity kernels for the
dynamic-topography objective in an instantaneous spherical-shell model.
Selected physical-property cell perturbations are validated with centered
finite differences. The objective compares the computed dynamic topography
against a prescribed 1000 m smooth spherical target function.

Run the benchmark from a build directory with:

```bash
./aspect ../benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical/adjoint_dynamic_topography.prm
```

Then create the cellwise density/viscosity kernel plot:

```bash
../benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical/scripts/plot_cellwise_kernels.py \
  output-adjoint-dynamic-topography-3d-spherical \
  --output ../benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical/doc/cellwise_density_viscosity_kernels.png
```

To run the finite-difference validation, first build the benchmark material
wrapper:

```bash
cmake -S ../benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical \
  -B adjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical_plugin \
  -D Aspect_DIR=$(pwd)
cmake --build adjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical_plugin
cp adjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical_plugin/libadjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical.release.so \
  ../benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical/
```

Then run:

```bash
../benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical/scripts/run_fd_validation.py \
  --aspect ./aspect \
  --selection quantile \
  --cells-per-property 16
```

The validation wrapper perturbs physical density or viscosity directly in a
selected active cell after material evaluation. Therefore the finite-difference
directional derivative is compared with the corresponding physical-property
adjoint kernel times cell volume.

========================================================================================
// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical/README.md
========================================================================================

========================================================================================
// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical/adjoint_dynamic_topography.prm
========================================================================================

# Benchmark for the instantaneous 3D dynamic-topography adjoint.
#
# This parameter file computes cellwise adjoint kernels for density and
# viscosity controls in a smooth 3D spherical-shell model. The initial
# temperature is 1600 K with a 100 K Gaussian anomaly near the top boundary.

set Dimension                              = 3
set CFL number                             = 1.0
set End time                               = 0
set Start time                             = 0
set Output directory                       = output-adjoint-dynamic-topography-3d-spherical
set Adiabatic surface temperature          = 1600
set Pressure normalization                 = surface
set Surface pressure                       = 0
set Use years instead of seconds           = false
set Nonlinear solver scheme                = no Advection, adjoint Stokes

subsection Adjoint
  set List of objectives = dynamic topography

  subsection Dynamic topography
    set Target topography model = function

    subsection Function
      set Coordinate system = spherical
      set Variable names = r,phi,theta
      set Function expression = 1000 * sin(phi) * cos(theta)
    end
  end
end

subsection Boundary temperature model
  set List of model names = spherical constant
  set Fixed temperature boundary indicators   = inner, outer

  subsection Spherical constant
    set Inner temperature = 1600
    set Outer temperature = 1600
  end
end

subsection Gravity model
  set Model name = radial constant

  subsection Radial constant
    set Magnitude = 10
  end
end

subsection Geometry model
  set Model name = spherical shell

  subsection Spherical shell
    set Inner radius = 3480000
    set Outer radius = 6336000
  end
end

subsection Initial temperature model
  set Model name = function

  subsection Function
    set Variable names     = x,y,z
    set Function expression = 1600 + 100 * exp(-(x*x + y*y + (z-6100000)*(z-6100000)) / (2 * 250000 * 250000))
  end
end

subsection Compositional fields
  set Number of fields = 0
end

subsection Material model
  set Model name = simple

  subsection Simple model
    set Reference temperature         = 1600
    set Viscosity                     = 1e21
    set Thermal viscosity exponent    = 36.84136
  end
end

subsection Mesh refinement
  set Initial adaptive refinement        = 0
  set Initial global refinement          = 1
end

subsection Boundary velocity model
  set Tangential velocity boundary indicators = inner, outer
end

subsection Solver parameters
  subsection Stokes solver parameters
    set Stokes solver type = block AMG
    set Linear solver tolerance = 1e-10
  end
end

subsection Postprocess
  set List of postprocessors = dynamic topography, adjoint kernels
end

========================================================================================
// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical/adjoint_dynamic_topography.prm

========================================================================================
// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical/cellwise_perturbation.cc
========================================================================================

/*
  Copyright (C) 2011 - 2024 by the authors of the ASPECT code.

  This file is part of ASPECT.

  ASPECT is free software; you can redistribute it and/or modify
  it under the terms of the GNU General Public License as published by
  the Free Software Foundation; either version 2, or (at your option)
  any later version.
*/

#include <aspect/material_model/interface.h>
#include <aspect/material_model/simple.h>
#include <aspect/simulator_access.h>

#include <cstdlib>

namespace aspect
{
  namespace MaterialModel
  {
    template <int dim>
    class AdjointCellwisePerturbation : public Simple<dim>
    {
      public:
        void
        evaluate(const MaterialModel::MaterialModelInputs<dim> &in,
                 MaterialModel::MaterialModelOutputs<dim> &out) const override;

        void
        parse_parameters(ParameterHandler &prm) override;

      private:
        int perturbed_active_cell_index;
        std::string perturbed_property;
        double perturbation_amplitude;
    };



    template <int dim>
    void
    AdjointCellwisePerturbation<dim>::evaluate(const MaterialModel::MaterialModelInputs<dim> &in,
                                               MaterialModel::MaterialModelOutputs<dim> &out) const
    {
      Simple<dim>::evaluate(in, out);

      if (perturbed_active_cell_index < 0
          || perturbation_amplitude == 0.0
          || in.current_cell.state() != IteratorState::valid
          || static_cast<int>(in.current_cell->active_cell_index()) != perturbed_active_cell_index)
        return;

      if (perturbed_property == "density")
        for (double &density : out.densities)
          density += perturbation_amplitude;
      else if (perturbed_property == "viscosity")
        for (double &viscosity : out.viscosities)
          viscosity += perturbation_amplitude;
    }


    template <int dim>
    void
    AdjointCellwisePerturbation<dim>::parse_parameters(ParameterHandler &prm)
    {
      Simple<dim>::parse_parameters(prm);

      const char *cell = std::getenv("ASPECT_ADJOINT_FD_CELL");
      const char *property = std::getenv("ASPECT_ADJOINT_FD_PROPERTY");
      const char *amplitude = std::getenv("ASPECT_ADJOINT_FD_AMPLITUDE");

      perturbed_active_cell_index = (cell != nullptr ? std::atoi(cell) : -1);
      perturbed_property = (property != nullptr ? property : "none");
      perturbation_amplitude = (amplitude != nullptr ? std::atof(amplitude) : 0.0);
    }
  }
}

namespace aspect
{
  namespace MaterialModel
  {
    ASPECT_REGISTER_MATERIAL_MODEL(AdjointCellwisePerturbation,
                                   "adjoint cellwise perturbation",
                                   "A benchmark material model that extends the simple model "
                                   "with an optional additive density or viscosity perturbation "
                                   "on one active cell. It is used only by the dynamic-topography "
                                   "adjoint finite-difference validation script.")
  }
}

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Plot cellwise dynamic-topography adjoint density and viscosity kernels."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import os
from pathlib import Path

os.environ.setdefault("MPLCONFIGDIR", str(Path(".matplotlib-cache").resolve()))
os.environ.setdefault("XDG_CACHE_HOME", str(Path(".cache").resolve()))

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import pandas as pd


def read_adjoint_table(path: Path, columns: list[str]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    with path.open() as stream:
        for line in stream:
            if not line.strip() or line.startswith("#"):
                continue
            rows.append(line.rstrip("\n").split("\t"))

    data = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=columns)
    for column in columns:
        if column not in {"objective", "term", "property", "control"}:
            data[column] = pd.to_numeric(data[column])
    return data


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument(
        "output_directory",
        type=Path,
        help="ASPECT output directory containing adjoint_kernels_rank_00000.txt",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output",
        type=Path,
        default=Path("doc/cellwise_density_viscosity_kernels.png"),
        help="Figure path to write.",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    kernel_file = args.output_directory / "adjoint_kernels_rank_00000.txt"
    kernels = read_adjoint_table(
        kernel_file,
        ["objective", "term", "property", "active_cell_index", "cell_volume", "value"],
    )

    terms = kernels[kernels["property"].isin(["density", "viscosity"])].copy()
    grouped = (
        terms.groupby(["property", "active_cell_index"], as_index=False)["value"]
        .sum()
        .sort_values(["property", "active_cell_index"])
    )

    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(9, 3.5), constrained_layout=True)
    for axis, property_name in zip(axes, ["density", "viscosity"]):
        subset = grouped[grouped["property"] == property_name]
        axis.axhline(0.0, color="0.3", linewidth=0.8)
        axis.plot(subset["active_cell_index"], subset["value"], marker="o", linewidth=1.2)
        axis.set_title(property_name)
        axis.set_xlabel("active cell index")
        axis.set_ylabel("cell-average kernel")

    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fig.savefig(args.output, dpi=200)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Run a cellwise finite-difference validation for the adjoint benchmark."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import math
import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

os.environ.setdefault("MPLCONFIGDIR", str(Path(".matplotlib-cache").resolve()))
os.environ.setdefault("XDG_CACHE_HOME", str(Path(".cache").resolve()))

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt


SCRIPT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
BENCHMARK_DIR = SCRIPT_DIR.parent


def replace_parameter(text: str, parameter: str, value: str) -> str:
    pattern = re.compile(rf"^(\s*set\s+{re.escape(parameter)}\s*=\s*).*$", re.MULTILINE)
    updated, count = pattern.subn(rf"\g<1>{value}", text, count=1)
    if count != 1:
        raise ValueError(f"Could not find unique parameter line for '{parameter}'.")
    return updated


def insert_plugin_material_model(text: str, plugin: Path) -> str:
    if "set Additional shared libraries" not in text:
        text = f"set Additional shared libraries            = {plugin}\n" + text
    else:
        text = replace_parameter(text, "Additional shared libraries", str(plugin))

    material_header = "subsection Material model\n  set Model name = simple"
    if material_header not in text:
        raise ValueError("Could not find the simple material model block.")
    text = text.replace(material_header,
                        "subsection Material model\n  set Model name = adjoint cellwise perturbation",
                        1)

    return text


def write_case_prm(
    base_text: str,
    prm_path: Path,
    output_directory: Path,
) -> None:
    text = replace_parameter(base_text, "Output directory", str(output_directory))
    prm_path.write_text(text)


def run_aspect(aspect: Path, prm_path: Path, log_path: Path, environment: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> None:
    aspect_executable = aspect.resolve() if aspect.exists() else aspect
    env = os.environ.copy()
    if environment is not None:
        env.update(environment)
    with log_path.open("w") as log:
        result = subprocess.run(
            [str(aspect_executable), str(prm_path)],
            stdout=log,
            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            text=True,
            env=env,
        )

    if result.returncode != 0:
        tail = "\n".join(log_path.read_text(errors="replace").splitlines()[-40:])
        raise RuntimeError(f"ASPECT failed for {prm_path}:\n{tail}")


def read_objective(output_directory: Path, objective_name: str) -> float:
    kernel_file = output_directory / "adjoint_kernels_rank_00000.txt"
    with kernel_file.open() as stream:
        for line in stream:
            fields = line.rstrip("\n").split("\t")
            if len(fields) == 3 and fields[0] == "# objective_value" and fields[1] == objective_name:
                return float(fields[2])
    raise ValueError(f"Could not find objective '{objective_name}' in {kernel_file}.")


def read_property_kernels(output_directory: Path) -> list[dict[str, float | str]]:
    kernel_file = output_directory / "adjoint_kernels_rank_00000.txt"
    contributions: dict[tuple[str, str, int], dict[str, float | str]] = {}
    with kernel_file.open() as stream:
        for line in stream:
            if not line.strip() or line.startswith("#"):
                continue
            objective, _term, property_name, cell_index, cell_volume, value = line.rstrip("\n").split("\t")
            if property_name not in {"density", "viscosity"}:
                continue

            key = (objective, property_name, int(cell_index))
            if key not in contributions:
                contributions[key] = {
                    "objective": objective,
                    "property": property_name,
                    "active_cell_index": int(cell_index),
                    "cell_volume": float(cell_volume),
                    "value": 0.0,
                }
            contributions[key]["value"] = float(contributions[key]["value"]) + float(value)

    return list(contributions.values())


def selected_kernel_rows(
    rows: list[dict[str, float | str]],
    objective_name: str,
    cells_per_property: int,
    selection: str,
    explicit_cells: list[int] | None,
) -> list[dict[str, float | str]]:
    selected: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
    for property_name in ["density", "viscosity"]:
        subset = [
            row for row in rows
            if row["objective"] == objective_name and row["property"] == property_name
        ]
        if explicit_cells is not None:
            selected.extend(row for row in subset if int(row["active_cell_index"]) in explicit_cells)
        else:
            if selection == "largest":
                selected.extend(
                    sorted(
                        subset,
                        key=lambda row: abs(float(row["value"]) * float(row["cell_volume"])),
                        reverse=True,
                    )[:cells_per_property]
                )
            else:
                sorted_subset = sorted(
                    subset,
                    key=lambda row: float(row["value"]) * float(row["cell_volume"]),
                )
                if cells_per_property >= len(sorted_subset):
                    selected.extend(sorted_subset)
                else:
                    selected_indices = {
                        round(i * (len(sorted_subset) - 1) / (cells_per_property - 1))
                        for i in range(cells_per_property)
                    }
                    selected.extend(sorted_subset[i] for i in sorted(selected_indices))
    return selected


def l2_relative_error(fd_values: list[float], adjoint_values: list[float]) -> float:
    numerator = sum((adjoint - fd) * (adjoint - fd)
                    for fd, adjoint in zip(fd_values, adjoint_values))
    denominator = sum(fd * fd for fd in fd_values)
    return math.sqrt(numerator / denominator) if denominator > 0.0 else 0.0


def pearson_correlation(fd_values: list[float], adjoint_values: list[float]) -> float:
    if len(fd_values) < 2:
        return 1.0

    mean_fd = sum(fd_values) / len(fd_values)
    mean_adjoint = sum(adjoint_values) / len(adjoint_values)
    covariance = sum((fd - mean_fd) * (adjoint - mean_adjoint)
                     for fd, adjoint in zip(fd_values, adjoint_values))
    variance_fd = sum((fd - mean_fd) * (fd - mean_fd) for fd in fd_values)
    variance_adjoint = sum((adjoint - mean_adjoint) * (adjoint - mean_adjoint)
                           for adjoint in adjoint_values)
    denominator = math.sqrt(variance_fd * variance_adjoint)
    return covariance / denominator if denominator > 0.0 else 1.0


def plot_results(rows: list[dict[str, float | str]], output: Path) -> None:
    plt.rcParams.update(
        {
            "font.size": 9,
            "axes.titlesize": 12,
            "axes.labelsize": 10,
            "xtick.labelsize": 8.5,
            "ytick.labelsize": 8.5,
            "legend.fontsize": 8.5,
            "axes.linewidth": 0.8,
        }
    )

    colors = {
        "density": "#2a9d8f",
        "viscosity": "#e76f51",
    }

    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(8.8, 4.0), constrained_layout=True)
    fig.patch.set_facecolor("white")

    for axis, control in zip(axes, ["density", "viscosity"]):
        subset = [row for row in rows if row["control"] == control]
        fd_values = [float(row["finite_difference"]) for row in subset]
        adjoint_values = [float(row["adjoint"]) for row in subset]
        relative_errors = [float(row["relative_error"]) for row in subset]

        axis.set_facecolor("#fbfbfa")
        axis.grid(True, which="major", color="#e7e3dd", linewidth=0.7)
        axis.axhline(0.0, color="#c8c4bd", linewidth=0.8, zorder=0)
        axis.axvline(0.0, color="#c8c4bd", linewidth=0.8, zorder=0)
        axis.scatter(fd_values,
                     adjoint_values,
                     s=42,
                     color=colors[control],
                     alpha=0.9,
                     edgecolor="white",
                     linewidth=0.7,
                     zorder=3)
        if fd_values and adjoint_values:
            raw_lower = min(fd_values + adjoint_values)
            raw_upper = max(fd_values + adjoint_values)
            if raw_lower >= 0.0:
                lower = 0.0
                upper = raw_upper * 1.08
            elif raw_upper <= 0.0:
                lower = raw_lower * 1.08
                upper = 0.0
            else:
                bound = max(abs(raw_lower), abs(raw_upper))
                lower = -1.08 * bound
                upper = 1.08 * bound
            axis.plot([lower, upper],
                      [lower, upper],
                      color="#202020",
                      linewidth=1.15,
                      label="1:1",
                      zorder=2)
            axis.set_xlim(lower, upper)
            axis.set_ylim(lower, upper)
            axis.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="box")

        annotation = (
            f"n = {len(subset)}\n"
            f"max rel. err. = {max(relative_errors):.2e}\n"
            f"L2 rel. err. = {l2_relative_error(fd_values, adjoint_values):.2e}\n"
            f"r = {pearson_correlation(fd_values, adjoint_values):.6f}"
        )
        axis.text(0.04,
                  0.96,
                  annotation,
                  transform=axis.transAxes,
                  va="top",
                  ha="left",
                  fontsize=8,
                  color="#333333",
                  bbox={
                      "boxstyle": "round,pad=0.32",
                      "facecolor": "white",
                      "edgecolor": "#ded8cf",
                      "linewidth": 0.7,
                      "alpha": 0.92,
                  })
        axis.set_title(control.capitalize(), pad=8)
        axis.set_xlabel("Finite difference")
        axis.set_ylabel("Adjoint kernel prediction")
        axis.ticklabel_format(axis="both", style="sci", scilimits=(-2, 3), useOffset=False)
        axis.legend(frameon=False, loc="lower right", handlelength=2.4)
        for spine in axis.spines.values():
            spine.set_color("#222222")

    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fig.savefig(output, dpi=220, facecolor=fig.get_facecolor())


def parse_cell_list(value: str | None) -> list[int] | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    return [int(item.strip()) for item in value.split(",") if item.strip()]


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--aspect", type=Path, default=Path("./aspect"), help="Path to the ASPECT executable.")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--plugin",
        type=Path,
        default=BENCHMARK_DIR / "libadjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical.so",
        help="Path to the benchmark material plugin.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--prm",
        type=Path,
        default=BENCHMARK_DIR / "adjoint_dynamic_topography.prm",
        help="Base benchmark prm file.",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--workdir", type=Path, default=Path("fd_validation_work"))
    parser.add_argument("--density-step", type=float, default=1.0)
    parser.add_argument("--viscosity-step", type=float, default=1.0e18)
    parser.add_argument("--objective", default="dynamic topography")
    parser.add_argument("--cells-per-property", type=int, default=16)
    parser.add_argument("--selection", choices=["quantile", "largest"], default="quantile")
    parser.add_argument("--cells", help="Comma-separated active cell indices. Defaults to the largest adjoint kernels.")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--csv",
        type=Path,
        default=BENCHMARK_DIR / "doc" / "fd_vs_adjoint_validation.csv",
        help="Validation table to write.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--figure",
        type=Path,
        default=BENCHMARK_DIR / "doc" / "fd_vs_adjoint_validation.png",
        help="Validation figure to write.",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    plugin = args.plugin.resolve()
    variant_plugin = plugin.with_name(plugin.name.replace(".so", ".release.so"))
    if not plugin.exists() and not variant_plugin.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Benchmark plugin not found: {plugin} or {variant_plugin}")

    base_text = insert_plugin_material_model(args.prm.read_text(), plugin)
    args.workdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    baseline_dir = args.workdir / "baseline"
    if baseline_dir.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(baseline_dir)
    baseline_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    write_case_prm(base_text, baseline_dir / "baseline.prm", baseline_dir / "output")
    run_aspect(args.aspect, baseline_dir / "baseline.prm", baseline_dir / "aspect.log")

    kernels = read_property_kernels(baseline_dir / "output")
    selected_rows = selected_kernel_rows(
        kernels,
        args.objective,
        args.cells_per_property,
        args.selection,
        parse_cell_list(args.cells),
    )

    rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
    for kernel in selected_rows:
        property_name = str(kernel["property"])
        cell_index = int(kernel["active_cell_index"])
        step = args.density_step if property_name == "density" else args.viscosity_step
        objectives: dict[str, float] = {}

        for sign_name, sign in [("minus", -1.0), ("plus", 1.0)]:
            case_name = f"{property_name}_{cell_index}_{sign_name}"
            case_dir = args.workdir / case_name
            if case_dir.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(case_dir)
            case_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
            write_case_prm(
                base_text,
                case_dir / f"{case_name}.prm",
                case_dir / "output",
            )
            run_aspect(
                args.aspect,
                case_dir / f"{case_name}.prm",
                case_dir / "aspect.log",
                {
                    "ASPECT_ADJOINT_FD_CELL": str(cell_index),
                    "ASPECT_ADJOINT_FD_PROPERTY": property_name,
                    "ASPECT_ADJOINT_FD_AMPLITUDE": f"{sign * step:.16g}",
                },
            )
            objectives[sign_name] = read_objective(case_dir / "output", args.objective)

        finite_difference = (objectives["plus"] - objectives["minus"]) / (2.0 * step)
        adjoint = float(kernel["value"]) * float(kernel["cell_volume"])
        scale = max(abs(finite_difference), abs(adjoint), 1.0e-300)
        rows.append(
            {
                "control": property_name,
                "active_cell_index": cell_index,
                "cell_volume": float(kernel["cell_volume"]),
                "step": step,
                "objective_minus": objectives["minus"],
                "objective_plus": objectives["plus"],
                "finite_difference": finite_difference,
                "adjoint": adjoint,
                "absolute_error": abs(finite_difference - adjoint),
                "relative_error": abs(finite_difference - adjoint) / scale,
            }
        )

    args.csv.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with args.csv.open("w", newline="") as stream:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(stream, fieldnames=list(rows[0].keys()))
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)

    plot_results(rows, args.figure)

    for row in rows:
        print(
            f"{row['control']} cell {row['active_cell_index']}: "
            f"FD={row['finite_difference']:.8e}, adjoint={row['adjoint']:.8e}, "
            f"relative_error={row['relative_error']:.3e}"
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_dynamic_topography_3d_spherical/scripts/run_fd_validation.py

// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_gadopt_mantle_convection/README.md

# TD-GADOPT: scaled G-ADOPT mantle-convection adjoint benchmark

This directory contains the durable configuration for the benchmark inserted
between O8 and O9. O9 sparse replay remains paused: the reference uses dense
trajectory storage (`Trajectory anchor spacing = 1`, cleanup policy `keep`).

`reference_forward.prm` is a serial 16 by 16 ASPECT smoke case with Q2/Q1
velocity-pressure, continuous Q1 temperature, 80 fixed ASPECT time steps of
`4`, and the G-ADOPT analytic initial-temperature field. Its physical
parameters are `rho = cp = H = alpha = g = eta = 1` and `k = 1e-6`, so
`kappa = k/(rho cp) = 1e-6` and `Ra = rho alpha g H^3/(eta kappa) = 1e6`.

The time and velocity conversions are

```
tau = H^2/kappa = 1e6,  U = kappa/H = 1e-6,
t* = t/tau = 1e-6 t,  u* = u/U = 1e6 u.
```

Thus ASPECT `dt = 4` represents G-ADOPT `dt* = 4e-6`; 80 steps end at
`t = 320`, or `t* = 3.2e-4`.

The target G-ADOPT tutorial uses a 150 by 150 extruded quadrilateral mesh,
CG2/CG1 velocity-pressure, DQ2 temperature, CG1 control, and implicit-midpoint
time integration. This ASPECT benchmark uses a 16 by 16 box mesh, Q2/Q1
velocity-pressure, continuous Q1 temperature, the current reduced Q1-like
initial-temperature control, and ASPECT's validated BDF-family transient path.
The temperature-space difference is intentional and is unrelated to the
velocity-pressure LBB (inf-sup) condition.

The temperature boundaries are strongly fixed to one at the bottom and zero at
the top. Tangential velocity boundary indicators on all four walls implement
the free-slip/impermeable condition. Side temperature boundaries are natural,
as in the reference specification.

`reference_forward_32.prm` is a refinement-5 overlay for the stability-qualified
32 by 32 truth trajectory. It leaves every physical and temporal setting from
the authoritative 16 by 16 input intact. The 16 by 16 Q1 run reached
`max(T80)=1.00647`; the 32 by 32 run retained `0 <= T <= 1` at every archived
step without artificial or entropy viscosity. Future inverse-observation work
uses the 32 by 32 trajectory unless a deliberate coarse-grid study is intended.

中文说明：本目录保存位于 O8 和 O9 之间的 TD-GADOPT 基准配置。O9 的稀疏
重放仍暂停；当前参考算例保留每一个时间步的稠密轨迹。`reference_forward.prm`
使用 16×16 串行网格、Q2/Q1 速度-压力、连续 Q1 温度，并以 ASPECT 的固定
时间步 `4` 运行 80 步。由于 `kappa=1e-6`，该时间步对应 G-ADOPT 的无量纲
`dt*=4e-6`，最终 `t*=3.2e-4`。温度空间的差异是明确记录的设计选择，与
速度-压力的 LBB／inf-sup 稳定性无关。`reference_forward_32.prm` 仅将细化
级别改为 5（32×32）；它消除了 16×16 在第 80 步的温度上冲，且未启用人工黏性。

## How to regenerate the retained reference and inversion evidence

The current source tree no longer contains the temporary G-ADOPT observation
import and accepted-iterate diagnostics. A retained local executable at
`/private/tmp/aspect-td-gadopt-diagnostics` still contains those hooks. The two
`*_legacy_diagnostics.prm` inputs preserve its pre-refactor parameter layout
and use its exact `g-adopt reference` initial-temperature plugin. They are
therefore the reproducibility path for the archived 32 by 32 experiment on
this workstation; they are not inputs for a newly built ASPECT executable.

Regenerate the 81 reference states, extract T70/T80 and u70...u79, run the
12-iteration inversion, and rebuild all enabled figures as follows:

```bash
cd benchmarks/adjoint_gadopt_mantle_convection

/private/tmp/aspect-td-gadopt-diagnostics \
  reference_forward_32_legacy_diagnostics.prm

python3 analysis/extract_reference_observations.py \
  --reference-dir output-adjoint-gadopt-reference-32 \
  --output-dir output-adjoint-gadopt-reference-32/diagnostics

/private/tmp/aspect-td-gadopt-diagnostics \
  inverse_terminal_temperature_10step_32_legacy_diagnostics.prm

conda run -n hang python analysis/make_all_plots.py \
  --output-dir output-adjoint-gadopt-inverse-terminal-10step-32
```

The canonical `inverse_terminal_temperature_10step_32.prm` remains a parameter
record rather than a standalone rerun recipe: the compatibility behavior is
enabled only by the explicitly named legacy input and retained executable.
The numerical record is
`output-adjoint-gadopt-inverse-terminal-10step-32/diagnostics/statistics_adjoint.csv`.
The accepted physical controls, terminal predictions, and figures are under
the neighboring `accepted_controls/`, `terminal_predictions/`, and `plots/`
directories. The CSV is authoritative; PNG figures are a presentation of
those data. Each terminal-only row reports `J_total = J_T`; velocity, damping,
and smoothing columns are intentionally absent until those objective terms
exist.

The authoritative 12-iteration terminal-only diagnostic run produced the
following selected accepted states; the full, one-row-per-accepted-iteration
record is `statistics_adjoint.csv`. `E_T_rel` is the FE-integrated
terminal-temperature error; `E_control_rel` is the physical initial-temperature
L2 error against T70, not the reduced-coordinate metric error.

| accepted iter | J/J0 | E_T_rel | E_u_rel | E_control_rel | \|\|g\|\|_L2 | active lower/upper |
| ---: | ---: | ---: | --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| 0 | 1.000000 | 0.374900 | N/A | 0.273663 | 0.286105 | 0 / 0 |
| 3 | 0.877528 | 0.351193 | N/A | 0.257148 | 0.268549 | 0 / 0 |
| 6 | 0.010949 | 0.039228 | N/A | 0.037110 | 0.026209 | 0 / 0 |
| 9 | 0.0000218 | 0.001749 | N/A | 0.003230 | 0.000851 | 0 / 0 |
| 12 | 0.000000417 | 0.000242 | N/A | 0.002521 | 0.0000849 | 0 / 0 |

中文说明：G-ADOPT 专用运行期诊断已经从当前核心 Manager 与 ROL 适配器中
删除，但本机保留的 `/private/tmp/aspect-td-gadopt-diagnostics` 可执行文件仍包含
原始观测导入和 accepted-iterate 输出 hook。两个 `*_legacy_diagnostics.prm`
文件与该旧参数树和精确初温插件配套，可以重建 32×32 reference、T70/T80、
u70…u79、12 次反演、VTK 和图片。canonical 的
`inverse_terminal_temperature_10step_32.prm` 仍只是参数记录，不应被视为当前
ASPECT 的独立反演配方。数值证据以
`diagnostics/statistics_adjoint.csv` 为准；`accepted_controls/`、
`terminal_predictions/` 和 `plots/` 分别保存已接受控制、终端预测和图像。
当前仅有终端温度目标，所以 `J_total=J_T`，速度误差 `E_u_rel` 标为 N/A，
而不是伪造为零。当前权威的 12 次迭代结果在第 12 次达到
`J/J0=4.17e-7`、`E_T_rel=2.42e-4` 和 `E_control_rel=2.52e-3`。表中的
`E_T_rel` 与 `E_control_rel` 都是有限元积分 L2 误差；后者在物理温度场中
对比 T70，不应与约化控制度量混淆。

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Extract TD-GADOPT truth CSV files from normal ASPECT VTU output.

This script is intentionally outside ASPECT's C++ runtime path.  It converts
ordinary visualization files into portable benchmark evidence and never
changes an adjoint objective, optimizer control, or trajectory archive.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
from pathlib import Path

from vtk_io import read_vtu


def write_temperature(path: Path, data: dict) -> None:
    with path.open("w", newline="") as output:
        writer = csv.writer(output)
        writer.writerow(("x", "y", "T"))
        for point, value in zip(data["Points"], data["T"].reshape(-1), strict=True):
            writer.writerow((f"{point[0]:.17g}", f"{point[1]:.17g}", f"{value:.17g}"))


def write_velocity(path: Path, data: dict) -> None:
    with path.open("w", newline="") as output:
        writer = csv.writer(output)
        writer.writerow(("x", "y", "u_x", "u_y", "u_z"))
        for point, value in zip(data["Points"], data["velocity"], strict=True):
            writer.writerow((f"{point[0]:.17g}", f"{point[1]:.17g}",
                             *(f"{component:.17g}" for component in value)))


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--reference-dir", type=Path, required=True,
                        help="ASPECT reference output directory containing solution/")
    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, required=True,
                        help="Destination diagnostics directory")
    arguments = parser.parse_args()
    # ASPECT's visualization lifecycle is the authoritative producer of the
    # fields.  Reading from ``solution/`` rather than an in-memory harness
    # makes the benchmark extraction reproducible from durable files.
    solution_dir = arguments.reference_dir / "solution"
    arguments.output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for step in range(70, 81):
        data = read_vtu(solution_dir / f"solution-{step:05d}.0000.vtu")
        if step in (70, 80):
            write_temperature(arguments.output_dir / f"reference_T{step}.csv", data)
        if step < 80:
            write_velocity(arguments.output_dir / f"reference_u{step}.csv", data)
    print(f"Wrote T70, T80, and u70...u79 CSV observations to {arguments.output_dir}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

========================================================================================
// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_gadopt_mantle_convection/analysis/extract_reference_observations.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Generate every TD-GADOPT plot supported by the available diagnostic data."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def run(script: str, output_dir: Path, required: Path | None = None) -> None:
    if required is not None and not required.exists():
        print(f"Skipping {script}: required data is absent: {required}")
        return
    command = [sys.executable, str(Path(__file__).with_name(script)), "--output-dir", str(output_dir)]
    completed = subprocess.run(command, text=True, capture_output=True)
    if completed.returncode:
        print(f"Skipping {script}: {completed.stderr.strip() or completed.stdout.strip()}")
    else:
        print(completed.stdout.strip() or f"Generated {script}")

def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(); parser.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args(); diagnostics = args.output_dir / "diagnostics"
    history = diagnostics / "statistics_adjoint.csv"
    if not history.exists():
        history = diagnostics / "iteration_history.csv"
    run("plot_iteration_history.py", args.output_dir, history)
    run("plot_initial_temperature_iterations.py", args.output_dir,
        diagnostics / "accepted_controls/truth_T70.vtu")
    run("plot_terminal_temperature_comparison.py", args.output_dir,
        diagnostics / "terminal_predictions/terminal_temperature_observed_T80.vtu")
    run("plot_inversion_storyboard.py", args.output_dir,
        diagnostics / "terminal_predictions/terminal_temperature_observed_T80.vtu")
    run("plot_velocity_misfit.py", args.output_dir)

if __name__ == "__main__": main()

// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_gadopt_mantle_convection/analysis/make_all_plots.py

"""Tiny SVG/PNG plotting helpers with no third-party plotting dependency."""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np


def colour(value: float, low: float, high: float, symmetric: bool = False) -> str:
    if symmetric:
        scale = max(abs(low), abs(high), 1.0e-30)
        q = max(0.0, min(1.0, 0.5 + 0.5 * value / scale))
        return f"rgb({int(255*q)},{int(255*(1-abs(2*q-1)))},{int(255*(1-q))})"
    q = max(0.0, min(1.0, (value-low) / max(high-low, 1.0e-30)))
    return f"rgb({int(40+210*q)},{int(20+180*q)},{int(150-110*q)})"


def heatmap(grid: np.ndarray, x: float, y: float, width: float, height: float,
            low: float, high: float, symmetric: bool = False) -> str:
    rows, columns = grid.shape
    cells: list[str] = []
    for row in range(rows - 1):
        for column in range(columns - 1):
            value = float(np.mean(grid[row:row+2, column:column+2]))
            left = x + width * column / (columns - 1)
            bottom = y + height * (rows - 2 - row) / (rows - 1)
            cells.append(f'<rect x="{left:.2f}" y="{bottom:.2f}" width="{width/(columns-1)+.2:.2f}" '
                         f'height="{height/(rows-1)+.2:.2f}" fill="{colour(value, low, high, symmetric)}"/>')
    return "".join(cells)


def svg_document(width: int, height: int, body: str) -> str:
    return (f'<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="{width}" height="{height}" '
            f'viewBox="0 0 {width} {height}"><rect width="100%" height="100%" fill="white"/>'
            '<style>text{font-family:Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif;fill:#111} .small{font-size:11px}'
            '.title{font-size:14px;font-weight:bold}</style>' + body + '</svg>')


def write_svg_png(svg: str, png: Path) -> None:
    png.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    svg_path = png.with_suffix(".svg")
    svg_path.write_text(svg)
    result = subprocess.run(["/usr/bin/qlmanage", "-t", "-s", "1200", "-o", str(png.parent), str(svg_path)],
                            capture_output=True, text=True)
    if result.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Quick Look could not create {png}: {result.stderr.strip()}")
    rendered = png.parent / (svg_path.name + ".png")
    if not rendered.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(f"Quick Look did not produce {rendered}")
    rendered.replace(png)
    svg_path.unlink()


def read_history(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, float | str]]:
    with path.open(newline="") as stream:
        rows = []
        for row in csv.DictReader(stream):
            rows.append({key: (float(value) if key not in ("update_type",) and value else value)
                         for key, value in row.items()})
    return rows

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Visualize true, initial, and accepted recovered TD-GADOPT temperatures."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import math
from pathlib import Path

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np

from vtk_io import scalar_grid


def accepted_fields(output: Path) -> list[tuple[str, Path, str]]:
    directory = output / "diagnostics/accepted_controls"
    entries = [("True T70", directory / "truth_T70.vtu", "truth_T70")]
    entries.extend((path.stem.replace("initial_temperature_", "accepted "), path, "initial_temperature")
                   for path in sorted(directory.glob("initial_temperature_iter_*.vtu")))
    return entries


def panel_figure(entries: list[tuple[str, Path, str]], output: Path, error: bool) -> None:
    truth = scalar_grid(entries[0][1], entries[0][2])[2]
    data = [(title, scalar_grid(path, field)[2]) for title, path, field in entries]
    if error:
        data = [(title, grid - truth) for title, grid in data[1:]]
        maximum = max(float(np.max(np.abs(grid))) for _, grid in data)
        vmin, vmax, cmap, name, heading = (-maximum, maximum, "coolwarm",
                                            "initial_temperature_error.png",
                                            "Recovered initial-temperature error")
    else:
        vmin, vmax, cmap, name, heading = (0.0, 1.0, "viridis",
                                            "initial_temperature_iterations.png",
                                            "Recovered initial temperature")
    columns = min(3, len(data))
    rows = math.ceil(len(data) / columns)
    figure, axes = plt.subplots(rows, columns, figsize=(4 * columns, 3.7 * rows),
                                layout="constrained", squeeze=False)
    image = None
    for axis, (title, grid) in zip(axes.flat, data):
        image = axis.imshow(grid, origin="lower", extent=(0, 1, 0, 1),
                            vmin=vmin, vmax=vmax, cmap=cmap)
        axis.set_title(title)
        axis.set_aspect("equal")
        axis.set_xlabel("x")
        axis.set_ylabel("y")
    for axis in axes.flat[len(data):]:
        axis.set_visible(False)
    figure.colorbar(image, ax=axes.flat[:len(data)], shrink=0.85,
                    label="T" if not error else r"$T_{ic}-T_{70}$")
    figure.suptitle(heading)
    figure.savefig(output / "diagnostics/plots" / name, dpi=180)
    plt.close(figure)


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    fields = accepted_fields(args.output_dir)
    panel_figure(fields, args.output_dir, False)
    panel_figure(fields, args.output_dir, True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

========================================================================================
// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_gadopt_mantle_convection/analysis/plot_initial_temperature_iterations.py

// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_gadopt_mantle_convection/analysis/plot_inversion_storyboard.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Create a paper-style TD-GADOPT initial/final reconstruction storyboard.

The figure uses only durable ASPECT VTU output produced by the validated
terminal-temperature inversion.  It deliberately lives outside ASPECT's C++
solver path: plotting must neither alter an optimization iterate nor require a
benchmark-specific postprocessor in the core executable.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from pathlib import Path

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np

from vtk_io import scalar_grid


SELECTED_ITERATIONS = (0, 2, 4, 6, 8, 12)


def field(path: Path, name: str) -> np.ndarray:
    """Read a scalar VTU field and fail early if archived evidence is absent."""
    if not path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Required ASPECT field does not exist: {path}")
    return scalar_grid(path, name)[2]


def add_panel(axis: plt.Axes, values: np.ndarray, *, cmap: str,
              vmin: float, vmax: float) -> plt.AxesImage:
    """Draw one square unit-box field with a quiet, publication-style frame."""
    image = axis.imshow(values, origin="lower", extent=(0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 1.0),
                        interpolation="nearest", cmap=cmap, vmin=vmin, vmax=vmax)
    axis.set_aspect("equal")
    axis.set_xticks(())
    axis.set_yticks(())
    for spine in axis.spines.values():
        spine.set_linewidth(0.55)
        spine.set_color("0.70")
    return image


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Plot reference and selected accepted TD-GADOPT iterates.")
    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, required=True,
                        help="Archived ASPECT inverse-output directory")
    parser.add_argument("--iterations", type=int, nargs="+", default=SELECTED_ITERATIONS,
                        help="Accepted iterations shown below the reference row")
    arguments = parser.parse_args()

    diagnostics = arguments.output_dir / "diagnostics"
    initial_directory = diagnostics / "accepted_controls"
    terminal_directory = diagnostics / "terminal_predictions"

    # The reference fields define a common temperature colour scale. Every
    # reconstruction is shown against the same [0,1] interval, so visual
    # improvement cannot be mistaken for an iteration-specific rescaling.
    true_initial = field(initial_directory / "truth_T70.vtu", "truth_T70")
    true_final = field(terminal_directory / "terminal_temperature_observed_T80.vtu",
                       "terminal_temperature")

    rows: list[tuple[str, np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]] = []
    for iteration in arguments.iterations:
        initial = field(initial_directory / f"initial_temperature_iter_{iteration:03d}.vtu",
                        "initial_temperature")
        final = field(terminal_directory / f"terminal_temperature_iter_{iteration:03d}.vtu",
                      "terminal_temperature")
        # Absolute error answers the presentation question directly: darker
        # gray means a larger pointwise mismatch, independent of its sign.
        rows.append((f"Iteration {iteration}", initial, np.abs(initial - true_initial),
                     final, np.abs(final - true_final)))

    maximum_misfit = max(float(np.max(values))
                         for row in rows for values in (row[2], row[4]))
    # Avoid a degenerate colourbar if a future exact reconstruction is plotted.
    maximum_misfit = max(maximum_misfit, np.finfo(float).eps)

    figure = plt.figure(figsize=(10.0, 2.22 * (len(rows) + 1) + 0.9),
                         layout="constrained")
    grid = figure.add_gridspec(len(rows) + 2, 4,
                               height_ratios=[1.0] * (len(rows) + 1) + [0.10])
    axes = np.empty((len(rows) + 1, 4), dtype=object)

    headings = ("Initial condition", "Initial misfit",
                "Final condition", "Final misfit")
    for column, heading in enumerate(headings):
        axis = figure.add_subplot(grid[0, column])
        axes[0, column] = axis
        axis.set_title(heading, fontsize=12, pad=12,
                       bbox={"facecolor": "white", "edgecolor": "black", "pad": 3.5})

    initial_image = add_panel(axes[0, 0], true_initial, cmap="turbo", vmin=0.0, vmax=1.0)
    axes[0, 1].axis("off")
    final_image = add_panel(axes[0, 2], true_final, cmap="turbo", vmin=0.0, vmax=1.0)
    axes[0, 3].axis("off")
    axes[0, 0].set_ylabel("Reference", rotation=90, labelpad=30, fontsize=11,
                          bbox={"facecolor": "white", "edgecolor": "black", "pad": 4})

    misfit_image = None
    for row_index, (label, initial, initial_misfit, final, final_misfit) in enumerate(rows, start=1):
        row_axes = [figure.add_subplot(grid[row_index, column]) for column in range(4)]
        axes[row_index, :] = row_axes
        initial_image = add_panel(row_axes[0], initial, cmap="turbo", vmin=0.0, vmax=1.0)
        misfit_image = add_panel(row_axes[1], initial_misfit, cmap="Greys",
                                 vmin=0.0, vmax=maximum_misfit)
        final_image = add_panel(row_axes[2], final, cmap="turbo", vmin=0.0, vmax=1.0)
        misfit_image = add_panel(row_axes[3], final_misfit, cmap="Greys",
                                 vmin=0.0, vmax=maximum_misfit)
        row_axes[0].set_ylabel(label, rotation=90, labelpad=30, fontsize=11,
                                bbox={"facecolor": "white", "edgecolor": "black", "pad": 4})

    # Two shared colourbars preserve comparability across all rows: temperature
    # is physical T in [0,1], whereas misfit is |T-T_reference| in [0,max].
    temperature_bar = figure.colorbar(initial_image, cax=figure.add_subplot(grid[-1, :2]),
                                      orientation="horizontal")
    temperature_bar.set_label("Temperature, $T$")
    misfit_bar = figure.colorbar(misfit_image, cax=figure.add_subplot(grid[-1, 2:]),
                                 orientation="horizontal")
    misfit_bar.set_label(r"Absolute misfit, $|T-T_{reference}|$")

    figure.suptitle("TD-GADOPT terminal-temperature inversion: accepted reconstructions",
                    fontsize=14)
    destination = diagnostics / "plots" / "inversion_storyboard.png"
    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    figure.savefig(destination, dpi=220)
    plt.close(figure)
    print(destination)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

========================================================================================
// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_gadopt_mantle_convection/analysis/plot_inversion_storyboard.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Plot accepted TD-GADOPT objective, errors, and reduced L2 gradient norm."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from pathlib import Path

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

from plot_common import read_history


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    history = args.output_dir / "diagnostics/statistics_adjoint.csv"
    if not history.exists():
        history = args.output_dir / "diagnostics/iteration_history.csv"
    rows = [row for row in read_history(history)
            if row["accepted"] == 1.0]
    if not rows:
        raise RuntimeError("iteration_history.csv has no accepted rows")
    iteration = [row["accepted_iteration"] for row in rows]
    panels = [("J_over_J0", r"$J_T/J_T(0)$"),
              ("terminal_T_error_rel", r"$E_T^{rel}$"),
              ("control_error_L2_rel", r"$E_{control}^{rel}$"),
              ("gradient_norm_L2", r"$\|g\|_{L^2}$")]
    figure, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(10, 7), layout="constrained")
    for axis, (column, label) in zip(axes.flat, panels, strict=True):
        axis.semilogy(iteration, [row[column] for row in rows], "o-", color="#1769aa")
        axis.set_xlabel("accepted iteration")
        axis.set_ylabel(label)
        axis.grid(True, which="both", alpha=0.3)
    figure.suptitle("TD-GADOPT terminal-temperature inversion history")
    figure.savefig(args.output_dir / "diagnostics/plots/iteration_history.png", dpi=180)
    plt.close(figure)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
