#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compare observed, initial, and final terminal-temperature fields."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from pathlib import Path

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np

from vtk_io import scalar_grid


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    directory = args.output_dir / "diagnostics/terminal_predictions"
    paths = sorted(directory.glob("terminal_temperature_iter_*.vtu"))
    if not paths:
        raise RuntimeError("No accepted terminal predictions were found")
    observed = scalar_grid(directory / "terminal_temperature_observed_T80.vtu", "terminal_temperature")[2]
    initial = scalar_grid(paths[0], "terminal_temperature")[2]
    final = scalar_grid(paths[-1], "terminal_temperature")[2]
    residual_limit = max(float(np.max(np.abs(initial-observed))),
                         float(np.max(np.abs(final-observed))))
    panels = [("Observed T80", observed, "viridis", 0.0, 1.0),
              ("Initial prediction", initial, "viridis", 0.0, 1.0),
              ("Final prediction", final, "viridis", 0.0, 1.0),
              ("Initial − observed", initial-observed, "coolwarm", -residual_limit, residual_limit),
              ("Final − observed", final-observed, "coolwarm", -residual_limit, residual_limit)]
    figure, axes = plt.subplots(2, 3, figsize=(12, 7), layout="constrained")
    for axis, (title, grid, cmap, vmin, vmax) in zip(axes.flat, panels):
        image = axis.imshow(grid, origin="lower", extent=(0, 1, 0, 1), cmap=cmap, vmin=vmin, vmax=vmax)
        figure.colorbar(image, ax=axis, shrink=0.8)
        axis.set(title=title, xlabel="x", ylabel="y", aspect="equal")
    axes.flat[-1].set_visible(False)
    figure.suptitle("TD-GADOPT terminal-temperature prediction comparison")
    figure.savefig(args.output_dir / "diagnostics/plots/terminal_temperature_comparison.png", dpi=180)
    plt.close(figure)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_gadopt_mantle_convection/analysis/vtk_io.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Small dependency-free reader for ASPECT's serial compressed VTU output."""

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import struct
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
import zlib
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np


_DTYPES = {
    "Float32": ("f", np.float32),
    "Float64": ("d", np.float64),
    "Int32": ("i", np.int32),
    "UInt8": ("B", np.uint8),
}


def _decode_binary(text: str, vtk_type: str) -> np.ndarray:
    """Decode VTK XML's vtkZLibDataCompressor block representation."""
    encoded = "".join(text.split())
    first = base64.b64decode(encoded[:8])
    blocks = struct.unpack("<I", first[:4])[0]
    header_bytes = (blocks + 3) * 4
    header_chars = 4 * ((header_bytes + 2) // 3)
    header = base64.b64decode(encoded[:header_chars])
    values = struct.unpack("<" + "I" * (blocks + 3), header)
    compressed_sizes = values[3:]
    payload = base64.b64decode(encoded[header_chars:])
    offset = 0
    raw = bytearray()
    for size in compressed_sizes:
        raw.extend(zlib.decompress(payload[offset:offset + size]))
        offset += size
    code, dtype = _DTYPES[vtk_type]
    del code
    return np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.dtype(dtype).newbyteorder("<")).copy()


def read_vtu(path: str | Path) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
    """Return Points and PointData arrays from one serial ASPECT VTU file."""
    root = ET.parse(path).getroot()
    piece = root.find("./UnstructuredGrid/Piece")
    if piece is None:
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: missing UnstructuredGrid/Piece")
    arrays: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
    point_array = piece.find("./Points/DataArray")
    if point_array is None:
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: missing Points/DataArray")
    npoints = int(piece.attrib["NumberOfPoints"])
    points = _decode_binary(point_array.text or "", point_array.attrib["type"])
    arrays["Points"] = points.reshape((npoints, int(point_array.attrib.get("NumberOfComponents", "3"))))
    point_data = piece.find("PointData")
    if point_data is not None:
        for array in point_data.findall("DataArray"):
            name = array.attrib.get("Name")
            if name and array.attrib.get("format") == "binary":
                data = _decode_binary(array.text or "", array.attrib["type"])
                arrays[name] = data.reshape((npoints, int(array.attrib.get("NumberOfComponents", "1"))))
    return arrays


def scalar_grid(path: str | Path, field: str) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Return x, y and a structured scalar grid from a regular ASPECT box VTU."""
    data = read_vtu(path)
    points = data["Points"]
    values = data[field].reshape(-1)
    x = np.unique(points[:, 0])
    y = np.unique(points[:, 1])
    grid = np.full((len(y), len(x)), np.nan)
    ix = {round(float(value), 12): index for index, value in enumerate(x)}
    iy = {round(float(value), 12): index for index, value in enumerate(y)}
    for point, value in zip(points, values, strict=True):
        grid[iy[round(float(point[1]), 12)], ix[round(float(point[0]), 12)]] = value
    if np.isnan(grid).any():
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: {field} is not a complete structured point grid")
    return x, y, grid

========================================================================================
// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_gadopt_mantle_convection/analysis/vtk_io.py

# Archived ten-transition terminal-temperature-only synthetic-twin input.

set Output directory = $ASPECT_SOURCE_DIR/benchmarks/adjoint_gadopt_mantle_convection/output-adjoint-gadopt-inverse-terminal-10step-32

subsection Termination criteria
  set End step = 10
end

subsection Adjoint
  set Solve mode = optimize

  subsection Optimization
    set Maximum iterations = 12
    set Initial trust-region radius = 1e-3
    set L-BFGS history size = 3
    set Use physical temperature bounds = true
  end
end

// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_gadopt_mantle_convection/reference_forward.prm

# TD-GADOPT reference-forward smoke case (16 x 16 cells).
#
# This is a scaled ASPECT reproduction of the G-ADOPT mantle-convection
# adjoint reference.
#
# Scaling:
#
#   rho = 1
#   cp  = 1
#   k   = 1e-6
#
# therefore
#
#   kappa = k / (rho * cp) = 1e-6.
#
# The G-ADOPT nondimensional timestep is
#
#   dt* = 4e-6.
#
# With H = 1 and kappa = 1e-6,
#
#   dt = dt* H^2 / kappa = 4.
#
# Therefore the ASPECT timestep below is 4, not 4e-6.
#
# Over 80 steps:
#
#   t_final = 80 * 4 = 320
#
# corresponding to the nondimensional duration
#
#   t_final* = t_final * kappa / H^2
#            = 3.2e-4.
#
# The adjoint trajectory is deliberately dense
# (trajectory anchor spacing = 1).
#
# O9 sparse replay is NOT enabled here.

set Dimension                              = 2
set Use years instead of seconds           = false
set Output directory                       = output-adjoint-gadopt-reference

set Pressure normalization                 = surface
set Surface pressure                       = 0

set Nonlinear solver scheme                = single Advection, single Stokes, adjoint temperature

# G-ADOPT uses dt* = 4e-6.
#
# Since the present ASPECT scaling has kappa = 1e-6 and H = 1,
# the corresponding ASPECT time increment is:
#
#   dt = dt* H^2 / kappa = 4.
set Maximum time step                      = 4

set CFL number                             = 1


subsection Discretization

  # Q2 velocity with the associated lower-order pressure space.
  #
  # The velocity-pressure pair, rather than the temperature space,
  # is the relevant pair for the LBB / inf-sup condition.
  set Stokes velocity polynomial degree = 2

  # Use continuous Q1 temperature for this ASPECT benchmark.
  set Temperature polynomial degree     = 1

end


subsection Time stepping

  # Force a deterministic fixed timestep.
  #
  # This corresponds to the G-ADOPT nondimensional timestep
  # dt* = 4e-6 under the present kappa = 1e-6 scaling.
  set List of model names = function

  subsection Function
    set Variable names       = time
    set Function expression  = 4
  end

end


subsection Termination criteria

  set Termination criteria = end step
  set End step             = 80

end


subsection Adjoint

  # This benchmark is currently a dense time-dependent reference case.
  # Sparse replay remains disabled until O9.
  set Solve mode = kernel


  set List of objectives = temperature field

  subsection Temperature field

    set Coordinate system = cartesian

    subsection Target function
      set Variable names       = x,y
      set Function expression  = 0
    end

    subsection Weight function
      set Variable names       = x,y
      set Function expression  = 1
    end

  end

  subsection Time dependent
    set Trajectory anchor spacing = 1
    set Trajectory cleanup policy = keep
  end
end


subsection Formulation

  set Formulation = Boussinesq approximation

end


subsection Geometry model

  set Model name = box

  subsection Box
    set X extent = 1
    set Y extent = 1
    set Z extent = 1
  end

end


subsection Initial temperature model

  # Use ASPECT's built-in parsed-function model. This is exactly the scaled
  # TD-GADOPT reference state, expressed directly in unit-box coordinates:
  # a smooth unit-thickness background layer plus a 0.1-amplitude Gaussian
  # anomaly centred at (0.5, 0.2) with standard deviation 0.1.
  #
  # Keeping this formula in the input file removes the need for a
  # benchmark-specific C++ initial-temperature plugin.
  set Model name = function

  subsection Function
    set Variable names = x,y
    set Function expression = 0.5*(erf((1-y)*3)+erf(-3*y)+1)+0.1*exp(-0.5*((x-0.5)^2+(y-0.2)^2)/(0.1^2))
  end

end


subsection Boundary temperature model

  set List of model names = box

  # Fixed vertical temperature contrast:
  #
  #   T_bottom = 1
  #   T_top    = 0
  #
  # Side boundaries retain the natural insulating condition.
  set Fixed temperature boundary indicators = bottom, top

  subsection Box
    set Bottom temperature = 1
    set Top temperature    = 0
  end

end


subsection Boundary velocity model

  # Impermeable / free-slip walls:
  #
  #   left/right: u_x = 0
  #   bottom/top: u_y = 0
  #
  # Tangential velocity remains unconstrained.
  set Tangential velocity boundary indicators = left, right, bottom, top

end


subsection Gravity model

  set Model name = vertical

  subsection Vertical
    set Magnitude = 1
  end

end


subsection Material model

  set Model name = simple

  subsection Simple model

    set Reference density             = 1
    set Reference specific heat       = 1
    set Reference temperature         = 0

    # kappa = k / (rho cp) = 1e-6.
    set Thermal conductivity          = 1e-6

    set Thermal expansion coefficient = 1

    set Viscosity                     = 1

  end

end


subsection Mesh refinement

  # 2^4 x 2^4 = 16 x 16 cells for the initial smoke benchmark.
  set Initial adaptive refinement        = 0
  set Initial global refinement          = 4
  set Time steps between mesh refinement = 0

end


subsection Checkpointing

  set Time between checkpoint  = 0
  set Steps between checkpoint = 0

end


subsection Postprocess

  set List of postprocessors =

end

// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_gadopt_mantle_convection/reference_forward.prm

========================================================================================
// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_geoid_density/G5_VALIDATION.md

# G5 non-self-gravitating geoid baseline

This is the acceptance record for the non-self-gravitating `geoid`
objective. It covers the shared mantle-density harmonic operator, the surface
and CMB dynamic-topography branches, and one summed physical Stokes adjoint.
It does not cover SG0 or any self-gravitating operator.

## Serial resource baseline

`/usr/bin/time -l build-release/aspect-release
benchmarks/adjoint_geoid_density/g5_cmb_m0.prm` completed with one forward and
one adjoint AMG solve (158 and 151 iterations), zero swaps, and a maximum RSS
of 315,719,680 bytes (about 301 MiB). A process sample independently measured
304,352 KiB RSS (about 297 MiB) and 436,332,480 bytes VSZ. The previous memory
report is therefore classified as:

```text
Transient/unreproduced host or UI memory observation
```

All G5 integration runs below were launched serially.

## Branch and solve-count matrix

| Case | Fixture | Result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| D | `adjoint_geoid_density.prm` | one forward GMG solve; no physical adjoint |
| T / D+T | `g5_surface_m0.prm` | 768 cells, forward + one AMG adjoint |
| B / D+B | `g5_cmb_m0.prm` | 768 cells, forward + one AMG adjoint |
| T+B / D+T+B | `g5_surface_cmb_m0.prm` | 768 cells, forward + one AMG adjoint |

The objective has no switch that removes `A_rho` from the forward observation
operator. “T”, “B”, and “T+B” mean that the density *control direction* is
held fixed, while the mandated mantle-density forward term remains present.

The surface and CMB `l=2,m=1` cosine and sine fixtures all completed on the
same 768-cell mesh, each with one forward and one physical adjoint solve.
They retain coverage of coefficient ordering, support-point projection, and
the inner-boundary sign/radial convention.

## Fixed-mesh full FD closure

The existing direct cellwise material wrapper selected the largest final
combined kernels: density cell 672 and raw-viscosity cell 644. Each entry is a
centered difference of the full D+T+B objective and the corresponding sum of
the direct mantle-density, surface-CBF, CMB-CBF, and implicit-Stokes kernels.

| Density step | Density relative error | Viscosity step | Viscosity relative error |
| ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| 0.1 | 2.84e-7 | 1e17 | 2.28e-7 |
| 1 | 2.82e-7 | 1e18 | 1.17e-6 |
| 10 | 2.62e-7 | 1e19 | 7.67e-5 |

The small and middle viscosity steps are the clear FD convergence region; the
large raw-viscosity step has the expected truncation error. The final kernel
output keeps the terms distinct:

```text
mantle-density geoid harmonic / density
dynamic topography surface objective / density, viscosity
dynamic topography CMB objective / density, viscosity
incompressible Stokes volume / density, viscosity
```

Current largest-kernel non-axisymmetric closures are surface `l=2,m=1`
cosine: density cell 682 `4.84e-7`, raw-viscosity cell 654 `4.09e-7`; and
CMB `l=2,m=1` sine: density cell 565 `5.06e-9`, raw-viscosity cell 711
`4.48e-7`. The CMB harmonic transpose is below `1e-15`; CMB m=0/cosine/sine
coefficient-to-FE dualities agree to printed precision; fixed-seed top+CMB
RHS superposition is about `1.0e-16` relative.

## AMR and MPI

The fixed mesh gives objective `7.374797682e-3`. A uniformly finer 6,144-cell
mesh gives `8.633180791e-3`, with the same complete direct/implicit
decomposition. A protected one-step adaptive mesh (minimum level 1 and no
coarsening) realizes 796 cells / 30,227 DoFs and completes the forward plus
one-adjoint pair before and after the initial adaptation.

For the adaptive mesh, a raw-viscosity FD retains the 796-cell realization and
closes at `1.97e-7`. A raw-density perturbation changes the AMR decision: the
baseline has 796 final cells while the perturbed runs remain at 768 cells.
That difference is a derivative through mesh selection and is explicitly out
of scope. G5 differentiates the observation operator on a realized mesh only;
it does not differentiate AMR decisions. The unprotected default AMR setup
also allows coarsening and is not an appropriate convergence comparison.

MPI ranks 2 and 4 are **NOT AVAILABLE** on this host: PRTE fails before
ASPECT starts because the host forbids binding its TCP listener. The 1-rank
comparison is the serial baseline above.

## State isolation and limits

The manager clears the forward pressure-normalization adjustment only while
solving the physical adjoint and restores it, together with the forward state,
RHS, linearization point, and constraints, on normal and exception paths.
The normal-path and forced-exception restoration checks are retained from G3/G4.
A dedicated two-objective sequential regression was not added.

No exact density-contrast cancellation is assumed: CBF uses local support
density, configured above/below density, local gravity, and a support-point
denominator, whereas geoid scaling uses the legacy boundary reference/mean
density, reference gravity, and radial factor.

G5 excludes self-gravity, coupled surface/CMB feedback, internal-interface
deformation, moving geometry and gravity derivatives, point-observation
synthesis, self-gravity matrix derivatives, and self-gravitating
boundary-density derivatives.

# Geoid adjoint baselines

This directory contains the G5 non-self-gravitating `geoid` baseline
and the production self-gravitating geoid baseline. The G5 objective uses the
shared degree-major cosine/sine layout and evaluates

```text
N = A_rho rho + B_t h_t + B_b h_b
J = 1/2 (N - N_obs)^T W (N - N_obs)
```

## G5 non-self-gravitating baseline

`adjoint_geoid_density.prm` is the fast density-only case (D). The
`g5_topography_base.prm` model is a compact fixed-mesh 3-D spherical-shell
case. Its leaf files provide the physical-adjoint cases:

| File | Enabled branches | Matrix cases covered |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `g5_surface_m0.prm` | surface | T and D+T |
| `g5_cmb_m0.prm` | CMB | B and D+B |
| `g5_surface_cmb_m0.prm` | surface and CMB | T+B and D+T+B |
| `g5_surface_m1_cos.prm`, `g5_surface_m1_sin.prm` | surface | permanent l=2,m=1 ordering checks |
| `g5_cmb_m1_cos.prm`, `g5_cmb_m1_sin.prm` | CMB | permanent l=2,m=1 ordering and inner-boundary checks |

The `_m0` files isolate l=2,m=0 with weights `1,0,0`; the cosine and sine
files isolate l=2,m=1 by using a unit observation and weight only in that
slot. The mantle-density forward operator is always part of this objective.
For cases labelled “no/fixed density”, hold the density control fixed in the
directional derivative; there is intentionally no switch that changes the
forward observation operator.

## Architecture and invariants

```text
GeoidObjective
  -> GeoidHarmonics: A_rho, B_t, B_b forward and transpose operators
  -> DynamicTopographyCBFTranspose: top and bottom CBF transposes
  -> one summed physical Stokes-adjoint RHS
```

The mantle-density transpose contributes the kernel label
`mantle-density geoid harmonic`. The CBF helper emits separate surface and
CMB density/viscosity labels. The manager uses no physical adjoint solve for
D, and exactly one for every objective with either topography branch; it sums
the top and bottom RHS contributions before that solve.

Pressure normalization is stateful in the forward solve. Before the physical
adjoint solve, the manager sets its stored forward pressure-normalization
adjustment to zero, then restores the saved forward solution, RHS,
linearization point, constraints, and adjustment on both normal and exception
paths. This prevents a forward pressure gauge from leaking into an adjoint
initial guess or a later objective.

## Operator conventions and contrast diagnostic

The CBF transpose uses the local support-point mantle density, the configured
density above (surface) or below (CMB), local gravity magnitude, and the
selected boundary's outward normal. Its pressure correction is constructed
with the same selected-boundary area and mean-pressure convention as the
forward dynamic-topography calculation.

The geoid harmonic scalings use the legacy boundary mean/reference density,
the configured density above/below, the surface/reference gravity, and the
appropriate radial factor. Consequently the CBF denominator and geoid
contrast are not the same discretized quantity. No exact density-contrast
cancellation is assumed or forced.

## Canonical checks

Run a fixed-mesh smoke case with, for example:

```sh
build-release/aspect-release benchmarks/adjoint_geoid_density/g5_surface_cmb_m0.prm
```

Expected solve counts are one forward / zero physical adjoint for D, and one
forward / one physical adjoint for every file with surface and/or CMB enabled.
Use the existing direct-control finite-difference wrapper from the 3-D
dynamic-topography benchmark for centered raw-density and raw-viscosity
directions. Sweep at least three epsilons and only report relative errors in
a resolved, non-near-zero region.

The permanent fast acceptance set is the three harmonic transpose identities,
top and CMB coefficient-to-FE seed dualities, and fixed-seed top+CMB RHS and
density/viscosity-kernel superposition. The permanent medium set is the
three branch configurations above plus `adjoint_geoid_density.prm`. The four
`m=1` files prevent regressions in cosine/sine ordering, support-point
projection, radial factors, and CMB signs.

Recorded G0–G4 fixed-mesh references are: CMB harmonic transpose relative
error below `1e-15`; CMB FE dualities for m=0, cosine, and sine agree to the
printed precision; and fixed-seed top+CMB RHS superposition has relative
error about `1.0e-16`. Robust CMB m=1-sine FD closure at cell 145 was
`2.13e-5` for density and `9.28e-6` for raw viscosity. These values are
baselines, not tolerances for bitwise comparison.

## Scope limits

G5 excludes self-gravitation, coupled surface/CMB geoid feedback,
internal-interface deformation, moving-geometry derivatives, and
self-gravity-matrix or self-gravitating boundary-density derivatives. It
differentiates the observation operator on the realized mesh only, not AMR
decisions or mesh selection. It also does not add point-observation
synthesis. MPI comparisons may differ in their final digits because of
reduction order; compare coefficients and directional derivatives
numerically, not bitwise.

## Self-gravitating production baseline

The self-gravitating objective is selected independently with:

```text
subsection Adjoint
  set List of objectives = self-gravitating geoid
  subsection Self-gravitating geoid
    ...
  end
end
```

It solves the SG0 coupled degree-wise system rather than modifying the G5
`geoid` path. Its public controls are the minimum/maximum degrees
(`l_min >= 2`), observed cosine/sine coefficient lists for surface/CMB
topography and geoid, and shared nonnegative degree/order weights.  The
fixed external `Surface density above` and `CMB density below` entries must
match the required `Postprocess/Dynamic topography` density-above/below
settings; a mismatch fails at parse/use time rather than silently changing a
CBF or matrix convention.

The final raw-density decomposition is:

```text
self-gravitating surface density moment
self-gravitating CMB density moment
dynamic topography surface objective
dynamic topography CMB objective
incompressible Stokes volume
self-gravity matrix density term
```

For raw viscosity, only the two CBF terms and `incompressible Stokes volume`
are present; the coupled 4x4 matrix has no viscosity derivative.

| Purpose | Canonical asset |
| --- | --- |
| SG forward, axisymmetric/non-axisymmetric | `sg0_coupled_m0.prm`, `sg0_coupled_m1.prm` |
| Production SG density/viscosity FD and one-adjoint invariant | `sg2_mixed.prm` plus the documented cellwise wrapper directions |
| Non-axisymmetric production SG density FD | `sg2_nonaxis_surface_geoid_m1.prm` |
| Four coupled basis-seed architecture regressions | `sg2_basis_*.prm` |
| Realized-mesh SG smoke | `sg4_amr_smoke.prm` |
| Operator ladder (SG0–SG3) | `build-release/aspect-release --test 'Self-gravitating*'` |
| Non-self-gravitating baseline | `g5_surface_cmb_m0.prm` |

The production reverse chain is `A_l^T z=q_x`, followed by the existing
top/CMB CBF and moment transposes, one summed physical Stokes adjoint RHS,
and the separate matrix-density contraction `-z^T(delta A_l)x`.  It does not
differentiate gravity, radii, geometry, moving interfaces, external-density
parameters, AMR decisions, log viscosity, or time dependence.

# SG0 coupled self-gravity forward-operator audit

## Reference

The audited reference is `self_gravity_hack26` at
`3b763d4a13d778bb6acdd421131585fcec2c80fe` (`self gravitation`). The configured
remotes expose `feature/self_gravity` and `self_gravity_hack26`; no exact
`geoid_self_gravitation` branch is present. Nothing was merged or
cherry-picked from that branch.

## Reconstructed forward system

For each real degree/order component the reference solves

```text
x = (s, b, Nt, Nb)

[ Dt gs, 0,       -rhot gs, 0       ] [s ]   [-taut]
[ 0,     Db gs,   0,        -Db gs  ] [b ] = [+taub]
[ -G R Dt, -G Rc (Rc/R)^(l+1) Db, 1, 0 ] [Nt]   [G It]
[ -G R (Rc/R)^l Dt, -G Rc Db, 0, 1 ] [Nb]   [G Ib]
```

where `Dt=rhot-rho_above`, `Db=rho_below-rhob`,
`G=4*pi*big_g/(gs*(2*l+1))`, `It=integral rho*r^l/R^(l+1)*Y dV`, and
`Ib=integral rho*Rc^l/r^(l+1)*Y dV`. The reference’s reduced stresses are
`tau=-CBF_traction.dot(outward_normal)-boundary_mean_pressure`; their top and
bottom RHS signs are respectively negative and positive. `Nt` and `Nb` are
geoid-height terms because the gravitational potential scaling is already
divided by surface gravity in `G`.

## SG0 implementation

`SelfGravitatingGeoidOperator` is a shared postprocess-level, forward-only
class. Its inputs explicitly contain the two density moments, top/bottom
reduced stresses, radii, surface gravity, boundary means, and exterior/interior
densities. Its result explicitly exposes surface/CMB topography and
surface/CMB geoid coefficients. It validates `l_min>=2`, factorizes/inverts a
4x4 matrix once per degree, solves cosine and sine RHS separately, and records
the maximum normalized `A*x-f` residual.

`compute_self_gravitating_density_moments()` reuses the G5
`SphericalHarmonicCoefficients` layout and MPI reduction, with raw (not
Gamma-scaled) `I_t` and `I_b` radial kernels. The layout is degree-major,
`m=0..l`, separate cosine/sine arrays, ASPECT fully normalized real harmonics
with Condon--Shortley phase, and `dV` volume quadrature. It has no parallel
coefficient layout.

Degrees zero and one are rejected deliberately: the audited coupled reference
only defines its system for `l>=2`.

## Forward reduced-stress provider and integration

`ForwardBoundaryReducedStressProvider` is now the G5-compatible forward
provider. It does not duplicate CBF assembly: it reads the documented velocity
block of `DynamicTopography::topography_vector()`, which is the existing CBF
traction. At the historical reference branch's Gauss face quadrature it
projects, independently on each boundary,

```text
tau = - CBF_traction . outward_normal - boundary_mean_pressure
tau_lm = integral(tau Y_lm dA / radius^2).
```

The provider obtains both mean pressures from `BoundaryPressures`, preserves
the outward CMB normal convention, uses the shared G5 degree-major harmonic
layout, and reduces each coefficient over MPI. Dynamic topography's stored
temperature component and its backward-advection sign adjustment are never
read: the CBF traction velocity block is instantaneous and unchanged by that
adjustment. This matches the reference's coupled path, which bypasses the
ordinary backward-advection topography conversion.

`compute_self_gravitating_geoid_harmonics()` connects density moments,
boundary densities, the provider, and the degree-wise solver. The registered
forward-only `self-gravitating geoid` postprocessor exposes every solved
surface/CMB topography and geoid cosine/sine coefficient through statistics,
so a separately built reference executable can be compared coefficient by
coefficient without changing either operator.

The reference parses surface/CMB inclusion flags but actually solves both
interfaces. SG0 intentionally has no such ineffective flags and always solves
the coupled two-boundary system.

## Validation results

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| Synthetic residual, l=2/3 matrix audit | **PASS** — 72 unit-test assertions; normalized residual tolerance `1e-10` |
| Synthetic cosine/sine isolation | **PASS** — l=2,m=0; l=2,m=1; l=3,m=2 are loaded in both components, and a bottom sine-only load leaves all cosine outputs zero |
| Limiting inputs | **PASS** — zero, top-only, bottom-only, and density-only inputs |
| Forward reduced-stress provider | **IMPLEMENTED** — existing DynamicTopography CBF traction plus both BoundaryPressures gauges |
| Canonical SG0 forward case | **PASS** — `sg0_coupled_m0.prm`: 768 cells, 28,690 DoFs, one 158-iteration Stokes solve, residual `2.62e-16` |
| Historical coefficient-level equivalence | **PASS** — independently instrumented `3b763d4a1` reference, matched 768-cell model; detailed results below |
| Non-axisymmetric integrated SG run | **PASS** — `sg0_coupled_m1.prm`: 768 cells, one 252-iteration solve, residual `3.55e-16`, and nonzero m=1 coefficients in statistics |
| G5 non-self-gravitating regression | **PASS** — `g5_surface_cmb_m0.prm`: 768 cells and the established 158/146 forward/adjoint solves |

## Historical-reference equivalence

The historical reference was built in the isolated worktree
`/private/tmp/aspect-sg-reference` at
`3b763d4a13d778bb6acdd421131585fcec2c80fe`. Only that worktree contains the
diagnostic patch and the translated fixture; neither was merged or copied into
the production source tree. The reference configuration was Release,
AppleClang 21 through `/opt/homebrew/bin/mpicxx`, deal.II 9.6.0, and one MPI
rank. Its bundled World Builder reported `USE_MPI=OFF`; the ASPECT reference
run itself was single-rank, matching the production comparison run.

The isolated fixture maps the current model directly: spherical shell
`R=6336000`, `Rc=3480000`; radial gravity `g_s=10`; the same simple material,
temperature, boundary conditions, refinement `(global, adaptive)=(1,0)`,
AMG tolerance, degree range `2..3`, and exterior/interior densities
`(0,9900)`. The only syntax translation is the historical solver spelling
`no Advection, single Stokes` for the current adjoint-Stokes one-forward-solve
configuration.

The historical diagnostic emitted every cosine/sine mode's
`tau_t,tau_b,I_t,I_b,s,b,N_t,N_b` at 17 significant digits. Current SG0 emits
the matching raw inputs, matrix scalars, and solved fields in `statistics`.

| Quantity | Maximum absolute difference | Maximum raw relative difference | Result |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| `tau_t` | 0 | 0 | **PASS** |
| `tau_b` | 0 | 0 | **PASS** |
| `I_t` | `4.045805109811653` | `5.418354407429308e-1` | **PASS — cancellation scale** |
| `I_b` | `4.722160085750666` | `3.591323791672586e-1` | **PASS — cancellation scale** |
| `A_l`, `l=2,3` | 0 | 0 | **PASS** |
| `s` | `8.037214839362028e-11` | `1.255321395801131e-1` | **PASS — cancellation scale** |
| `b` | `1.214089630088962e-10` | `1.513885242040572e-2` | **PASS — cancellation scale** |
| `N_t` | `8.037214839361863e-11` | `2.043707975924164e-1` | **PASS — cancellation scale** |
| `N_b` | `1.214089630088954e-10` | `3.284167979605641e-2` | **PASS — cancellation scale** |

`I_t/I_b` agree at the physically relevant scale: for example the historical
and current `l=2,m=0` surface moments are respectively
`-7.128923599008513e10` and `-7.128923598739932e10`. The apparent large raw
relative errors occur only in symmetry-forbidden non-axisymmetric remnants
whose independently summed values are O(1--10) after cancellation of
O(1e10) quadrature contributions. They produce at most `1.22e-10` difference
in any solved coefficient. This is numerical cancellation, not a harmonic,
traction, sign, matrix, or convention discrepancy.

# SG1 coupled spectral-transpose validation

## Scope

SG1 adds only the fixed-matrix spectral reverse solve to
`SelfGravitatingGeoidOperator`:

```text
A_l^T z = q_x,
q_x = (q_s, q_b, q_Nt, q_Nb).
```

`apply_transpose()` takes arbitrary four-field harmonic seeds and returns the
four named components of `z`, plus named physical RHS-channel seeds:

```text
dJ/d tau_t = -z_s
dJ/d tau_b = +z_b
dJ/d I_t   = Gamma_l z_Nt
dJ/d I_b   = Gamma_l z_Nb.
```

The shared `matrix_for_degree()` is the only construction path for the SG0
matrix. Forward solves factorize `A_l` once per degree; SG1 factorizes `A_l^T`
once per degree. Neither path factorizes separately by order or real channel.

## Deliberate boundary

SG1 does not apply a CBF-traction transpose, a density-moment transpose, a
physical Stokes adjoint, a material kernel, parameter derivatives, or
`-z^T (delta A_l) x`. It holds `A_l` fixed and ends at the four spectral
physical-channel seeds above.

## Tests

`unit_tests/self_gravitating_geoid_operator.cc` uses deterministic nonzero
four-component `q_x` and physical perturbations for every retained real mode:

| Degree | Orders | Channels | Identity checked |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2 | 0, 1, 2 | cosine, sine | `q_x^T delta_x = seed_tau_t delta_tau_t + seed_tau_b delta_tau_b + seed_It delta_It + seed_Ib delta_Ib` |
| 3 | 0, 1, 2, 3 | cosine, sine | same |

For all 14 degree/order/channel cases, the tests independently reconstruct
`A_l`, verify every component of `A_l^T z-q_x` to a scale-aware `1e-10`
tolerance, verify the bilinear identity to the same tolerance, and directly
check each of the four RHS-seed signs/scalings. The SG test filter reports:

```text
All tests passed (199 assertions in 3 test cases)
```

## Regression

The serial Release G5 `g5_surface_cmb_m0.prm` regression remains unchanged:
768 active cells, 28,690 DoFs, and the established 158/146 forward/adjoint
Stokes solves. `git diff --check` passes.

## Status

```text
Generic A_l^T spectral solve: PASS
Arbitrary four-field q_x: PASS
RHS tau/I seed signs and Gamma scaling: PASS
Direct transpose residual: PASS
Bilinear transpose identity: PASS
CBF/density/physical-adjoint propagation: NOT IMPLEMENTED (out of SG1 scope)
delta A material/parameter term: NOT IMPLEMENTED (out of SG1 scope)
G0-G5 regression: PASS
SG1 status: PASS
```

No subsequent SG stage was started.

# SG2 fixed-matrix physical transpose propagation

## Scope

SG2 connects the SG1 coupled spectral seeds to the existing physical adjoint
operators while deliberately treating the degree-wise coupled matrix as
fixed.  It does **not** include `-z^T (delta A_l) x`, boundary-density/
contrast derivatives in `A_l`, gravity/radius derivatives, moving interfaces,
log-viscosity, AMR-decision derivatives, or time dependence.

`SelfGravitatingGeoidObjective` is a coefficient-space quadratic test and
production-ready objective:

```text
J = 1/2 sum W (x-x_obs)^2,
x = (surface topography, CMB topography, surface geoid, CMB geoid).
```

It first applies the SG1 `A_l^-T` solve.  The four resulting physical-channel
seeds are then handled as follows:

```text
-z_s       -> surface reduced-stress harmonic transpose -> top CBF transpose
+z_b       -> CMB reduced-stress harmonic transpose     -> bottom CBF transpose
Gamma z_Nt -> surface volume-density-moment transpose
Gamma z_Nb -> CMB volume-density-moment transpose
```

The two CBF calls add to one `rhs`, so the objective manager executes one
physical Stokes adjoint solve, never one per boundary or spectral field.

## Exact physical interfaces

`assemble_boundary_reduced_stress_transpose()` is the reusable reverse of the
SG0 harmonic projection.  For a traction coefficient seed `q_tau`, it uses
the same real-harmonic convention and Gauss face quadrature as SG0 and forms

```text
q_tau dOmega = q_tau/r^2 dA,
q_h = q_tau (rho-rho_exterior) |g| / r^2.
```

Thus `q_h` is precisely the temperature-support-point FE seed expected by
the validated `DynamicTopographyCBFTranspose`, and its independently
accumulated pressure seed is `integral q_tau/r^2 dA`.  This preserves the
reduced-stress sign and mean-pressure transpose at both the surface and CMB;
no second CBF implementation was added.

`compute_self_gravitating_density_moment_transpose()` uses the exact SG0
volume quadrature, global degree-major real harmonic layout, and cell-average
kernel convention.  The two radial kernels are separate in code:

```text
I_t: r^l / R^(l+1)
I_b: Rc^l / r^(l+1)
```

The final kernel output keeps the requested decomposition:

```text
self-gravitating surface density moment / density
self-gravitating CMB density moment     / density
dynamic topography surface objective    / density, viscosity
dynamic topography CMB objective        / density, viscosity
incompressible Stokes volume            / density, viscosity
```

There is no explicit density-moment viscosity term and no log-viscosity
conversion.

## Serial validation

All runs used the established fixed 768-cell / 28,690-DoF spherical-shell
model and were launched serially.

`sg2_mixed.prm`, which has nonzero coupled seeds in all four solved fields,
completed with one 158-iteration forward Stokes solve and one 147-iteration
physical adjoint solve.  The native output contains all five contributions
listed above.

The `sg2_basis_*.prm` fixtures use SG0's native l=2 coefficients as their
observations except for one coefficient shifted by one.  Therefore each is an
exact unit `q_x` basis seed and has `J=0.5` (surface-topography output shows
`0.5000000000000009`, the remaining four show `0.5`):

| Fixture | Seed | Forward / adjoint iterations |
|---|---|---|
| `sg2_basis_surface_topography.prm` | `(1,0,0,0)` | 158 / 147 |
| `sg2_basis_cmb_topography.prm` | `(0,1,0,0)` | 158 / 151 |
| `sg2_basis_surface_geoid.prm` | `(0,0,1,0)` | 158 / 146 |
| `sg2_basis_cmb_geoid.prm` | `(0,0,0,1)` | 158 / 149 |
| `sg2_nonaxis_surface_geoid_m1.prm` | surface-geoid `l=2,m=1` cosine | 158 / 100 |

The `m=1` case is deliberately non-axisymmetric and exercises both the
harmonic-to-FE boundary assembly and the moment kernels.

For a frozen-matrix interior cell (active cell 672), the existing
benchmark-only additive material wrapper was used only through stdin-fed
temporary parameters.  Both top/bottom boundary densities remained printed
as 3300 kg/m3, so no boundary scalar entering `A_l` was perturbed.  Centered
differences of the complete SG2 objective gave:

| Control | Step | FD | summed direct + implicit kernel | Relative difference |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| raw density | 1 kg/m3 | -128.35704594 | -128.374 | 1.3e-4 |
| raw viscosity | 1e18 Pa s | 3.41085965e-22 | 3.41999e-22 | 2.7e-3 |

The visibly asymmetric one-sided density changes are normal at this step;
the centered derivative is the comparison.  This check is intentionally on
an interior control and therefore excludes SG3's matrix-material term.

## Regression

* SG0 axisymmetric: 768 cells, one 158-iteration solve, coupled residual
  `2.62e-16`.
* SG0 non-axisymmetric: 768 cells, one 252-iteration solve, residual
  `3.55e-16`.
* SG1 unit tests: all 199 assertions in 3 self-gravitating test cases pass.
* G5 combined non-SG regression: unchanged 768-cell, 158/146
  forward/adjoint pattern.
* `git diff --check`: pass.

MPI remains unavailable on this host because PRTE cannot bind its local TCP
listener before ASPECT starts.  No manager logic changed, and the G5
pressure-normalized regression therefore remains the pressure-gauge isolation
check.  AMR was not added: SG2 differentiates the fixed realized mesh only.

## Final status

```text
Bottom traction spectral -> FE transpose: PASS
Top traction seed duality: PASS
Bottom traction seed duality: PASS
I_t density transpose: PASS
I_b density transpose: PASS
```

# SG3 coupled-matrix raw-density derivative

## Matrix dependency audit

The audited SG0 construction for each degree is

```text
A(0,0) = Dt gs                 A(0,2) = -rho_t gs
A(1,1) = Db gs                 A(1,3) = -Db gs
A(2,0) = -Gamma R Dt           A(2,1) = -Gamma Rc (Rc/R)^(l+1) Db
A(3,0) = -Gamma R (Rc/R)^l Dt  A(3,1) = -Gamma Rc Db
```

where `Dt=rho_t-rho_above`, `Db=rho_below-rho_b`, and
`Gamma=4*pi*G/(gs*(2l+1))`.

| Matrix quantity | SG3 treatment |
|---|---|
| `rho_t`, `rho_b` boundary mantle means | differentiated raw-density controls |
| `Dt`, `Db` | differentiated through their production definitions |
| `rho_above`, `rho_below` | fixed external parameters |
| `gs`, `R`, `Rc`, `Gamma` | fixed gravity/geometry quantities |

In particular, the bottom contrast derivative is negative:
`d Db/d rho_b = -1`.  The explicit `-rho_t gs` entry and the top contrast
entries are both included, so top-density dependence is not double counted or
silently dropped.

## Implementation

`SelfGravitatingGeoidOperator` now exposes the two exact per-degree
`dA/d rho_bar` matrices and accumulates, across every degree/order and real
cosine/sine component,

```text
dJ/d rho_t = -sum z^T (dA/d rho_t) x
dJ/d rho_b = -sum z^T (dA/d rho_b) x.
```

No inverse or factorization derivative is used.

`BoundaryDensities` defines the forward means as area-weighted face-density
integrals.  Its transpose follows that exact discretization: `top`/`bottom`
boundary IDs; `introspection().face_quadratures.temperature`; face `JxW`;
division by the globally MPI-reduced boundary area; and cell-average kernel
storage.  Geometry and area variation are held fixed.  The resulting new,
separate raw-density contribution is named:

```text
self-gravity matrix density term
```

It is added once, independently of the two density moments, CBF direct
terms, and implicit Stokes kernel.  There is no matrix-viscosity contribution.

## Operator validation

`unit_tests/self_gravitating_geoid_operator.cc` now checks, for both top and
bottom means at degrees 2 and 3:

* every entry of the production `dA` against an independent construction;
* centered finite differences of `q^T x(A)` with RHS fixed, using both
  cosine and sine channels;
* linear superposition of independently seeded matrix contractions.

The Release self-gravity test filter reports **269 assertions in 5 cases**.

## Physical fixed-mesh finite differences

All integration runs were serial on the validated 768-cell / 28,690-DoF
model.  The benchmark-only additive material wrapper was supplied only via
temporary stdin parameters; no generated or production source was changed.

| Case | FD | complete density kernel | Relative difference |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Interior cell 672, density step 1 | -128.35704594 | -128.374 | 1.3e-4 |
| CMB-adjacent cell 3, density step 0.01 | 38.23145771 | 38.2232 | 2.2e-4 |
| Surface-adjacent cell 423, density step 0.01 | 99.97159583 | 99.9519 | 2.0e-4 |
| Non-axisymmetric `l=2,m=1` surface-geoid, cell 508, density step 0.01 | 7.51951767 | 7.51929 | 3.0e-5 |
| Interior cell 672, raw viscosity step 1e18 Pa s | 3.41085965e-22 | 3.41999e-22 | 2.7e-3 |

The matrix kernel is exactly zero at interior cell 672.  At the selected CMB
and surface cells its integrated values are respectively `-9.67288785e-6`
and `7.61150812e-6`, confirming its boundary localization and opposite
mean-density chains.  Matrix contractions also pass independent operator
superposition tests before this physical chaining is applied.

Raw viscosity is unchanged from SG2 because `A_l` has no viscosity input.
Gravity, radii, geometry, external densities, AMR decisions, and log
viscosity remain out of scope.

## Regression

* SG0 m=0: 768 cells, one 158-iteration solve, residual `2.62e-16`.
* SG0 m=1: 768 cells, one 252-iteration solve, residual `3.55e-16`.
* SG1/SG3 operator tests: 269 assertions in 5 cases pass.
* SG2 mixed: one 158-iteration forward plus one 147-iteration adjoint solve.
* G5 combined non-SG: unchanged 158/146 forward/adjoint pattern.
* `git diff --check`: pass.

MPI remains unavailable because PRTE cannot bind its local TCP listener before
ASPECT starts.  No manager code changed; established pressure-gauge isolation
is therefore preserved.  AMR is intentionally differentiated only on a
realized mesh and was not part of this fixed-mesh acceptance suite.

## Final status

```text
A_l density dependencies audited: YES

Independent dA entry tests: PASS

-z^T(dA)x operator-level FD: PASS

Top boundary mean-density transpose: PASS

CMB boundary mean-density transpose: PASS

Interior perturbation matrix term: ZERO

Surface-adjacent density matrix derivative: PASS

CMB-adjacent density matrix derivative: PASS

Top + CMB matrix-term superposition: PASS

Full physical density FD: PASS

Non-axisymmetric full density FD: PASS

Raw-viscosity FD preserved: PASS

Matrix viscosity derivative: ZERO

Gravity/radius derivatives: NOT IMPLEMENTED

SG0 forward preserved: PASS

SG1 spectral transpose preserved: PASS

SG2 fixed-A_l validation preserved: PASS
```

# SG4 final self-gravity integration validation

SG4 makes the already implemented SG0--SG3 chain the production
`self-gravitating geoid` adjoint objective. It intentionally adds no new
gravity, geometry, or moving-boundary physics.

## Production interface and derivative convention

The objective is selected with `List of objectives = self-gravitating geoid`.
It uses a single coupled degree-wise 4-by-4 solve, with harmonic state order
`[h_s, h_c, N_s, N_c]`. `Minimum spherical harmonic degree` is restricted to
at least two. The public fixed-external values `Surface density above` and
`CMB density below` are checked against the required Dynamic topography
postprocessor values. They are external constants, rather than inversion
controls. A deliberate surface-density mismatch was rejected with the
explicit required-Dynamic-topography-density diagnostic; no ASPECT/MPI
process remained after that expected failure.

The complete raw-density kernel is the sum of the following separately named
contributions:

```text
self-gravitating surface density moment
self-gravitating CMB density moment
dynamic topography surface objective
dynamic topography CMB objective
incompressible Stokes volume
self-gravity matrix density term
```

The reverse path is the coupled spectral transpose, CBF and boundary-moment
transposes, one summed physical Stokes-adjoint right-hand side, and the
explicit matrix contraction `-z^T (delta A_l) x`. The matrix term includes
the fixed-geometry boundary mean-density convention. Viscosity differentiates
only through the physical CBF/Stokes path.

## Fixed-mesh production checks

All runs below used the Release executable and ran serially. The standard
model has 768 active cells and 28,690 degrees of freedom. Each production
case reports one forward and one adjoint Stokes solve.

| Case | Result |
| --- | --- |
| `sg2_mixed.prm` | PASS; 158 forward and 147 adjoint AMG iterations |
| `sg2_basis_surface_topography.prm` | PASS; `J = 0.5000000000000009`; 158/147 iterations |
| `sg2_basis_cmb_topography.prm` | PASS; `J = 0.5`; 158/151 iterations |
| `sg2_basis_surface_geoid.prm` | PASS; `J = 0.5`; 158/146 iterations |
| `sg2_basis_cmb_geoid.prm` | PASS; `J = 0.5`; 158/149 iterations |
| `sg2_nonaxis_surface_geoid_m1.prm` | PASS; `J = 0.5`; 158/100 iterations |
| `sg0_coupled_m0.prm`, `sg0_coupled_m1.prm` | PASS; 28,690 DoFs in both cases |
| `build-release/aspect-release --test 'Self-gravitating*'` | PASS; 269 assertions in 5 test cases |

The unit/operator ladder retains the SG0 coupled-forward residuals of
`2.62e-16` (axisymmetric) and `3.55e-16` (non-axisymmetric), as well as the
SG1 spectral, SG2 physical, and SG3 matrix-derivative identities.

## Centered finite differences

The documented cellwise material-model wrapper was used only for these
validation runs; it is not part of the production source. `sg2_mixed.prm`
was restored and rerun without that wrapper after the checks.

| Direction and centered perturbation | Finite difference | Integrated raw kernel | Relative difference |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| surface-adjacent density cell 423, `+/- 0.1` | `99.97159579` | `99.9519` | `1.97e-4` |
| surface-adjacent density cell 423, `+/- 0.01` | `99.97159583` | `99.9519` | `1.97e-4` |
| surface-adjacent density cell 423, `+/- 0.001` | `99.97159607` | `99.9519` | `1.97e-4` |
| CMB-adjacent density cell 3, `+/- 0.01` | `38.23195771` | `38.2232` | `2.3e-4` |
| interior density cell 672, `+/- 1` | `-128.35704594` | `-128.374` | `1.3e-4` |
| raw viscosity cell 672, `+/- 1e18` Pa s | `3.41085965e-22` | `3.41999e-22` | `2.7e-3` |
| raw viscosity cell 672, `+/- 1e17` Pa s | `3.41089338e-22` | `3.41999e-22` | `2.7e-3` |
| non-axisymmetric surface density cell 508, `+/- 0.01` | `7.51951767` | `7.51929` | `3.0e-5` |

The boundary-localized density directions exercise the surface/CMB moment
and the SG3 matrix-density term in addition to the physical adjoint path.
The raw-viscosity directions verify that no spurious direct matrix derivative
is introduced.

## Mesh, gauge, resources, and regressions

`sg4_amr_smoke.prm` completed with one initial adaptive-refinement cycle. The
realized mesh has 796 active cells and 30,227 DoFs; its final forward/adjoint
solves used 193/146 AMG iterations. The preparatory solve on the initial mesh
is the normal AMR mesh-selection pass and is not an additional objective
adjoint.

The production path uses the existing manager pressure-gauge save/reset/
restore logic, unchanged from the validated G5 physical-adjoint path. No
SG-specific gauge state or debug output remains.

`/usr/bin/time -l build-release/aspect-release
benchmarks/adjoint_geoid_density/sg2_mixed.prm` completed normally in 5.72 s
elapsed (ASPECT reported 3.85 s). This macOS environment denied the
`kern.clockrate` query used by `time -l`, so it emitted no maximum-RSS value;
peak RSS is therefore **not available**, rather than estimated. Runs remained
bounded at the ordinary fixed-mesh problem size.

The single-rank regression `g5_surface_cmb_m0.prm` also passes at 28,690
DoFs. Multi-rank validation is **not available** in this host sandbox because
PRTE/Open MPI cannot create its local TCP listener (`Operation not permitted`);
this is an environment restriction, not an SG4 numerical result.

## Final acceptance

| Requirement | Status |
| --- | --- |
| production coupled forward/transpose and physical propagation | PASS |
| explicit matrix material derivative and density FD closure | PASS |
| raw-viscosity FD closure | PASS |
| non-axisymmetric coupled FD/ordering case | PASS |
| basis seeds, one-forward/one-adjoint invariant, and pressure gauge | PASS |
| AMR realized-mesh smoke | PASS |
| Release resource smoke | PASS, RSS measurement unavailable on this OS |
| MPI comparison | NOT AVAILABLE in sandbox |
| non-self-gravitating G5 regression | PASS |

Scope remains fixed-geometry, fixed-external-density, fixed-gravity/radius
linearization on the realized mesh. It excludes moving interfaces, geometry
or gravity derivatives, AMR-decision derivatives, time-dependent adjoints,
and point-observation synthesis.

# Density-only coefficient-space geoid adjoint benchmark.
# The nonzero temperature field produces a nonzero density harmonic signal;
# observations are zero so q_N is nontrivial.

set Dimension                              = 3
set End time                               = 0
set Start time                             = 0
set Output directory                       = output-adjoint-geoid-density
set Adiabatic surface temperature          = 0
set Nonlinear solver scheme                = no Advection, adjoint Stokes

subsection Adjoint
  set List of objectives = geoid

  subsection Geoid
    set Minimum spherical harmonic degree = 0
    set Maximum spherical harmonic degree = 2
    set Observed cosine coefficients = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
    set Observed sine coefficients   = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
    set Coefficient weights          = 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6
  end
end

subsection Material model
  set Model name = simple

  subsection Simple model
    set Thermal expansion coefficient = 0.1
    set Reference temperature         = 0
    set Reference density             = 1
    set Viscosity                     = 1
  end
end

subsection Discretization
  set Stokes velocity polynomial degree = 2
end

subsection Geometry model
  set Model name = spherical shell

  subsection Spherical shell
    set Inner radius = 200000
    set Outer radius = 400000
  end
end

subsection Boundary velocity model
  set Tangential velocity boundary indicators = inner, outer
end

subsection Nullspace removal
  set Remove nullspace = angular momentum
end

subsection Initial temperature model
  set Model name = function

  subsection Function
    set Coordinate system = spherical
    set Function constants = pi=3.1415926536
    set Variable names = r,phi,theta,t
    set Function expression = (5/2)*sqrt(5/pi)*(3*(cos(theta))^2-1) + 0.2*sin(phi)*sin(theta)
  end
end

subsection Gravity model
  set Model name = radial constant

  subsection Radial constant
    set Magnitude = 9.8
  end
end

subsection Mesh refinement
  set Initial global refinement = 1
  set Initial adaptive refinement = 0
end

subsection Postprocess
  set List of postprocessors = geoid, adjoint kernels

  subsection Geoid
    set Minimum degree = 0
    set Maximum degree = 2
    set Include surface topography contribution = false
    set Include CMB topography contribution = false
    set Output density anomaly contribution coefficients = true
  end
end

# Shared fixed-mesh model for the G5 non-self-gravitating geoid cases.
# Individual files in this directory include this model and select the
# harmonic seed and enabled topography branches.

set Dimension                              = 3
set CFL number                             = 1.0
set End time                               = 0
set Start time                             = 0
set Output directory                       = output-adjoint-geoid-g5-base
set Adiabatic surface temperature          = 1600
set Pressure normalization                 = surface
set Surface pressure                       = 0
set Use years instead of seconds           = false
set Nonlinear solver scheme                = no Advection, adjoint Stokes

subsection Adjoint
  set List of objectives = geoid

  subsection Geoid
    set Minimum spherical harmonic degree = 2
    set Maximum spherical harmonic degree = 2
    set Observed cosine coefficients = 0, 0, 0
    set Observed sine coefficients = 0, 0, 0
    set Coefficient weights = 1, 2, 3
    set Include surface dynamic topography contribution = false
    set Surface density above = 0
    set Include CMB dynamic topography contribution = false
    set CMB density below = 9900
  end

  subsection Dynamic topography
    set Target topography model = function

    subsection Function
      set Coordinate system = spherical
      set Variable names = r,phi,theta
      set Function expression = 1000 * sin(phi) * cos(theta)
    end
  end
end

subsection Boundary temperature model
  set List of model names = spherical constant
  set Fixed temperature boundary indicators = inner, outer

  subsection Spherical constant
    set Inner temperature = 1600
    set Outer temperature = 1600
  end
end

subsection Gravity model
  set Model name = radial constant

  subsection Radial constant
    set Magnitude = 10
  end
end

subsection Geometry model
  set Model name = spherical shell

  subsection Spherical shell
    set Inner radius = 3480000
    set Outer radius = 6336000
  end
end

subsection Initial temperature model
  set Model name = function

  subsection Function
    set Variable names = x,y,z
    set Function expression = 1600 + 100 * exp(-(x*x + y*y + (z-6100000)*(z-6100000)) / (2 * 250000 * 250000))
  end
end

subsection Compositional fields
  set Number of fields = 0
end

subsection Material model
  set Model name = simple

  subsection Simple model
    set Reference temperature = 1600
    set Viscosity = 1e21
    set Thermal viscosity exponent = 36.84136
  end
end

subsection Mesh refinement
  set Initial adaptive refinement = 0
  set Initial global refinement = 1
end

subsection Boundary velocity model
  set Tangential velocity boundary indicators = inner, outer
end

subsection Solver parameters
  subsection Stokes solver parameters
    set Stokes solver type = block AMG
    set Linear solver tolerance = 1e-10
  end
end

subsection Postprocess
  set List of postprocessors = dynamic topography, boundary densities, adjoint kernels
end

# Production fixed-matrix coupled self-gravity integration case. All four
# observed coefficient fields are zero, so its quadratic seed exercises the

    set Minimum spherical harmonic degree = 2
    set Maximum spherical harmonic degree = 2
    set Observed surface topography cosine coefficients = 0, 0, 0
    set Observed surface topography sine coefficients = 0, 0, 0
    set Observed CMB topography cosine coefficients = 0, 0, 0
    set Observed CMB topography sine coefficients = 0, 0, 0
    set Observed surface geoid cosine coefficients = 0, 0, 0
    set Observed surface geoid sine coefficients = 0, 0, 0
    set Observed CMB geoid cosine coefficients = 0, 0, 0
    set Observed CMB geoid sine coefficients = 0, 0, 0
    set Coefficient weights = 1, 2, 3
  end
end

subsection Postprocess
  set List of postprocessors = dynamic topography, boundary densities, adjoint kernels
end

# Curved free-slip regression. The full 2-D annulus forces deal.II to express
# u.n = 0 as affine relations between Cartesian velocity components.

include $ASPECT_SOURCE_DIR/benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature.prm

set Output directory = output-adjoint-bc-curved-free-slip

subsection Adjoint

  subsection Diagnostics
    set Run C to Stokes RHS transpose test = true
    set C to Stokes RHS transpose test epsilon = 1e-7
  end
end

subsection Geometry model
  set Model name = spherical shell

  subsection Spherical shell
    set Inner radius = 1
    set Outer radius = 2
    set Opening angle = 360
  end
end

subsection Boundary temperature model
  set List of model names = spherical constant
  set Fixed temperature boundary indicators = inner, outer

  subsection Spherical constant
    set Inner temperature = 0.5
    set Outer temperature = 0.5
  end
end

subsection Boundary velocity model
  set Tangential velocity boundary indicators = inner, outer
end

subsection Gravity model
  set Model name = radial constant

  subsection Radial constant
    set Magnitude = 1
  end
end

// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_bc_fixed_heat_flux.prm
========================================================================================

# Mixed thermal-boundary regression for the time-dependent adjoint: fixed
# temperature at the top and a state-independent, nonzero conductive heat
# flux at the impermeable bottom. The inherited velocity boundaries are free
# slip, so this exercises the discrete natural boundary path without adding
# an adjoint temperature Dirichlet constraint at the flux boundary.

include $ASPECT_SOURCE_DIR/benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature.prm

set Output directory = output-adjoint-bc-fixed-heat-flux

subsection Adjoint

  subsection Diagnostics
    set Run transposed advection constraint test = true
  end
end

subsection Boundary temperature model
  set Fixed temperature boundary indicators = top
end

subsection Boundary heat flux model
  set Fixed heat flux boundary indicators = bottom
  set Model name = function

  subsection Function
    set Function expression = -0.02
  end
end

// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_bc_fixed_heat_flux.prm
========================================================================================

========================================================================================
// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_bc_inflow_composition.prm
========================================================================================

# EXPECTED FAILURE / unsupported v1 diagnostic. The bottom is a persistent
# inflow with C_D(t)=0.5+0.1 sin(2 pi t), so u.n != 0 and composition Dirichlet
# data depend on the inflow set. The snapshot correctly replays the accepted
# constraint graph, but the advection transpose boundary term is intentionally
# outside Boundary Conditions v1. The runtime guard must reject this input.

include $ASPECT_SOURCE_DIR/benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_time_dependent_composition_objective_no_av.prm

set Output directory = output-adjoint-bc-inflow-composition
set Maximum time step = 0.01

subsection Adjoint

  subsection Diagnostics
    set Run transposed advection constraint test = true
  end
end

subsection Boundary velocity model
  set Tangential velocity boundary indicators = left, right, top
  set Prescribed velocity boundary indicators = bottom: function

  subsection Function
    set Variable names = x, y, t
    set Function expression = 0; 0.01
  end
end

subsection Boundary composition model
  set Fixed composition boundary indicators = bottom
  set Allow fixed composition on outflow boundaries = false
  set List of model names = function

  subsection Function
    set Variable names = x, y, t
    set Function expression = 0.5 + 0.1*sin(2*pi*t)
  end
end

// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_bc_inflow_composition.prm

# Nonzero, component-wise prescribed tangential velocity on the top boundary.
# The bottom is no slip and the side walls retain free slip. In the reverse
# solve the stored constraint graph is homogenized, so the top x component is
# zero for the adjoint even though the forward value is nonzero.

include $ASPECT_SOURCE_DIR/benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature.prm

set Output directory = output-adjoint-bc-prescribed-velocity

subsection Boundary velocity model
  set Tangential velocity boundary indicators = left, right
  set Zero velocity boundary indicators = bottom
  set Prescribed velocity boundary indicators = top x: function

  subsection Function
    set Variable names = x, y, t
    set Function expression = 0.1; 0
  end
end

# Time-dependent counterpart of adjoint_bc_prescribed_velocity.prm. The

  end
end

# Expected-failure benchmark for v1 MaterialModelAdjoint pressure safety.
# The material model generates bar_p through pressure-dependent density in the
# temperature material-prefactor path; pressure scattering is intentionally
# unsupported in v1.

set Output directory = output-adjoint-material-pressure-unsupported

subsection Formulation
  set Formulation = custom
  set Mass conservation = incompressible
  set Temperature equation = real density
end

subsection Material model
  set Model name = adjoint pressure material model

  subsection Adjoint pressure material model
    set Reference density = 1
    set Reference specific heat = 1
    set Pressure density coefficient = 1e-6
    set Viscosity = 1
    set Thermal conductivity = 1e-6
  end
end

========================================================================================
// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_restart_and_trajectory_lifecycle.md
========================================================================================

# Adjoint restart and trajectory lifecycle v1

This checkpoint is deliberately separate from ASPECT's ordinary forward
restart checkpoint. The forward checkpoint resumes `Y_n -> Y_(n+1)`; this one
resumes an already-started reverse recurrence.

## Reverse-state audit

| State | Checkpointed | Reconstructible | Reason |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Current, previous, previous-previous temperature adjoints | yes | no | BDF and stabilization transpose can seed two earlier levels. |
| The same three levels for every composition adjoint | yes | no | Temperature material-prefactor and composition transpose modify them. |
| Current, previous, previous-previous Stokes-adjoint RHS | yes | no | Velocity/stabilization transpose accumulates RHS before the Stokes transpose. |
| Three-state forward cache | no | yes | Rebuilt from retained trajectory records. |
| Constraints, matrices, preconditioners, material outputs, entropy-viscosity intermediates | no | yes | Reconstructed from fixed mesh, recorded state and time. |
| Terminal objective values | yes | no | Preserves reported values without reevaluating terminal objectives. |
| Initial gradients | no, until completion | yes | They are the final pending reverse state. |
| `KernelRepository` | no | not accumulated | The current transient sweep does not add kernels during reverse. |
| Plugin-hidden reverse scalar state | no known state | n/a | The audited calculators have no persistent reverse scalar state. |

`next_reverse_step` is the next seed index to process. History level zero is
that index; levels one and two are its previous and previous-previous levels.
After processing step `r`, the checkpoint therefore stores
`next_reverse_step = r - 1`. One operation can only modify these three pending
levels.

## MPI restart contract

The v1 MPI implementation is validated for a new `mpirun` with the **same
MPI rank count, mesh, DoF numbering, and ownership partition** as the run that
wrote the archive. Every rank writes and restores only its own owned reverse
state. Restore checks the stored process count and the owned global DoF indices
in every rank-local vector payload.

It does not support a different rank count, repartitioning, a changed mesh, or
forward replay across a different execution layout. The trajectory itself uses
shared MPI-I/O segments; the reverse checkpoint remains rank-local payloads
plus root-published metadata. MatrixFree data, GMG hierarchies, matrices,
transfers, constraints, and preconditioners remain runtime-only state and are
never checkpointed.

## Transaction and cleanup

For checkpoint generation `N`, rank zero creates `checkpoint_N.tmp`, all ranks
write and close their `rank_XXXXX.bin` payload, and a collective success check
must pass before rank zero writes shared metadata. Rank zero then renames the
directory to `checkpoint_N`, and only after that collective result succeeds
does it publish `latest`. Every stage is followed by a collective decision, so
a rank-local failure leads every rank down the same controlled termination
path. A `.tmp` directory is never restored.

Before `latest` is published, restart selects the prior generation even if a
newly named checkpoint directory exists. After `latest` is published, the new
generation is authoritative. This distinction is intentional: the durable
restart publication point is `latest`, not the earlier directory rename.

For a checkpoint with `next_reverse_step = n`, the next reverse operation
reconstructs records `n-1`, `n-2`, and `n-3` (where present). Safe-incremental
cleanup therefore retains `0..n-1` and may reclaim `[n,last]`. The checkpoint
stores this cleanup frontier. On resume, cleanup is repeated to the committed
frontier before any remaining record is read, so removal interrupted part-way
through is idempotent and cannot make the committed reverse state unrecoverable.

Under MPI, logical unavailability is first published in the shared trajectory
manifest. Rank zero then removes a `trajectory_XXXXXX.bin` segment only when
every record it contains is obsolete; there is no compaction and no rank-local
trajectory payload file. A missing fully obsolete segment is a safe no-op,
which makes repeated cleanup and recovery from a partial cleanup safe.

`Trajectory cleanup policy = safe incremental` is the default. `on success`
retains all temporary data until final gradients are written. `keep` retains
archive, manifest, and restart generations. No policy depends on a destructor,
so an exception retains recoverable data.

The direct-solver lifecycle controls first exercised serial safe-incremental
commit, cleanup, interruption, and recovery at reverse step 10. The MPI M2--M4
fixtures extend this to two ranks. They use a tiny 268-DoF case, a persistent
guarded `mpirun -n 2` session, and a 1 GiB RSS kill guard (with `vmmap`/`sample`
capture from 500 MiB). The observed peak guarded total RSS was about 288 MiB.

| Milestone | Validation | Result |
|---|---|---|
| M2 | Mid-sweep stop at `next_reverse_step = 10`, then a fresh 2-rank resume | Gradient is textually identical to the uninterrupted two-rank baseline. |
| M3 | A rank-1 injected failure after tmp creation, payload write, collective payload success, metadata write, directory commit, and `latest` publication | Pre-publication failures resume generation 3 at step 15; the post-`latest` failure resumes generation 4 at step 10. No run deadlocked or restored a `.tmp` directory. |
| M4 | Safe-incremental stop/resume and failures before cleanup, after rank-local deletion, before cleanup reduction, before manifest publication, and after manifest publication | Every fresh 2-rank resume completed with a textually identical gradient. |

For the fixture, the baseline gradient L2 norm is
`0.013480703099710863`, the terminal objective is
`0.004837524602356089`, and every validated MPI resume had relative L2 error
and maximum absolute difference equal to zero. `on success` retains all data
during reverse and removes it collectively only after final gradients are
written. `keep` retains its 21-record archive and restart data after success.
`safe incremental` performs collective final archive removal after successful
gradient output. No policy depends on a destructor, so abnormal termination
leaves restartable data.

Fresh trajectory creation resets an existing directory only when it contains a
trajectory manifest (or legacy metadata signature); an unknown directory is
rejected rather than recursively deleted.

## Scope and limitations

## Sparse-anchor restart qualification

For sparse spacing `K`, a reverse checkpoint at `next_reverse_step = r`
requires the lower anchor of the interval containing record `r-1`. The
2026-09-08 direct-Stokes qualification verified K=5 at `r=13` (anchor 10) and
`r=10` (anchor 5), in serial and with two ranks. Each restart rebuilt the
five-transition disposable interval and reproduced the uninterrupted gradient
byte-for-byte. Safe-incremental cleanup retained the required anchor before
removing obsolete shared segments; injected failures after rank-local payload
deletion and before/after manifest publication recovered idempotently from the
authoritative checkpoint.

The active entropy-viscosity K=5 case also restarted at `r=5`, retained anchor
0, replayed five transitions, and reproduced the uninterrupted gradient
byte-for-byte. This does not broaden the fixed-mesh/same-layout contract.

MPI restart and lifecycle safety are validated only under the contract above.
Different rank counts, repartitioning, sparse checkpointing, Revolve, AMR
replay, and new adjoint physics are separate future milestones. The current
work uses finite shared-MPI trajectory segments, but does not reduce
forward-end peak archive size or change validated adjoint PDE algebra.

## Matrix-free cold-rebuild audit (R0)

The missing cold-resume dependency was not a matrix, MatrixFree object, MG
hierarchy, or preconditioner. `Simulator::pressure_scaling` is initialized to a
signaling NaN in the constructor and is intentionally not checkpointed. A normal
forward Stokes solve calls `compute_pressure_scaling_factor()` immediately before
assembly. The adjoint-resume branch skips that solve, so the first reconstructed
GMG pressure mass operator saw NaN scaling and produced a non-finite diagonal.

`Manager::add_stokes_field_transpose()` now recomputes that scalar from the
restored primal snapshot immediately before its primal-topology Stokes assembly.
This is the same runtime preparation as the normal solve, and serializes no
solver state.

`adjoint_restart_r0_cold_rebuild.prm` is the A/B test. It invalidates MatrixFree
and GMG DoF/preconditioner state immediately before the first reverse Stokes
solve, then lets the ordinary reconstruction path rebuild it. It completed the
first rebuild with positive pressure-mass diagonals on all three levels, ruling
out a generally invalid clean GMG rebuild (case A). The resumed R0 run initially
reported identical DoF and constraint counts but `nonfinite=4` on level 0; after
the pressure-scaling reconstruction it reports zero nonpositive/nonfinite entries
on every level and completes the reverse sweep.

With `Solver parameters/Matrix Free/Output cold rebuild diagnostics = true`,
the build emits `ASPECT_MF_GMG_LEVEL` and `ASPECT_MF_MASS_DIAGONAL` lines with
level DoF/owned/relevant counts, actual level constraint counts, viscosity table
shape, pressure scaling, component, nonpositive/nonfinite diagonal counts,
minimum positive entry, and the first offending pressure DoF.

The direct-solver control fixtures (`adjoint_restart_r0_direct_{baseline,stop,
resume}.prm`) exercised a step-10 mid-sweep interruption and recovery. The
resumed and uninterrupted initial-temperature gradient files were byte-identical
(relative L2 error 0). The analogous GMG resumed and uninterrupted R0 gradients
were also byte-identical after the pressure-scaling reconstruction.

## Runtime-state audit categories

Sparse replay/restart work classifies state deliberately: persistent physical
state (trajectory records), persistent adjoint state (the reverse checkpoint),
reconstructible runtime state (current constraints, linearization context, and
`pressure_scaling`), and derived solver state (matrices, MatrixFree data, MG
hierarchy, transfer, and preconditioners). `pressure_scaling` is in the third
category: it must be recomputed from the restored physical state, but must not
be serialized. The fourth category remains entirely runtime-reconstructed.

# Temperature-control benchmark for cp(T) in the direct temperature material
# prefactor path. Density temperature dependence is disabled to isolate cp(T).

set Additional shared libraries = $ASPECT_SOURCE_DIR/benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/plugin/libadjoint_material_model.so

include $ASPECT_SOURCE_DIR/benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature.prm

set Output directory = output-adjoint-temperature-cp-t-direct

subsection Material model
  set Model name = adjoint material model

  subsection Adjoint material model
    set Reference density = 1
    set Reference specific heat = 1
    set Reference temperature = 0
    set Thermal expansion coefficient = 0
    set Specific heat temperature coefficient = 2e-3
    set Density differential for compositional field 1 = 0
    set Viscosity = 1
    set Thermal conductivity = 1
  end
end

// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_temperature_cp_t_direct.prm

  subsection Adjoint material model

    set Reference specific heat = 1
    set Reference temperature = 0
    set Thermal expansion coefficient = 0

    set Density differential for compositional field 1 = 0
    set Viscosity = 1

# Active-EV eta-only benchmark for C -> eta(C) -> u -> nu_EV -> T objective.

include $ASPECT_SOURCE_DIR/benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_temperature_eta_c_stokes_no_av.prm

set Output directory = output-adjoint-temperature-eta-c-stokes-ev

subsection Adjoint

  subsection Diagnostics
    set Run direct Stokes viscosity derivative test = true
    set Direct Stokes viscosity derivative test epsilon = 1e-7
    set Run artificial viscosity coefficient seed test = true
    set Artificial viscosity coefficient seed test epsilon = 1e-7
    set Run artificial viscosity estimator transpose test = true
    set Artificial viscosity estimator transpose test epsilon = 1e-11
  end
end

  subsection Stabilization parameters
    set cR   = 1, 1
    set beta = 0.5, 0.5
  end
end

========================================================================================
// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_temperature_eta_c_stokes_no_av.prm
========================================================================================

# Coupled one-material-path benchmark for C -> eta(C) -> Stokes -> T objective.
# Density and cp derivatives are disabled so the composition gradient isolates
# the Stokes viscosity-coefficient route.

set Additional shared libraries = $ASPECT_SOURCE_DIR/benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/plugin/libadjoint_material_model.so

include $ASPECT_SOURCE_DIR/benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature_composition_coupled_no_av.prm

set Output directory = output-adjoint-temperature-eta-c-stokes-no-av

subsection Solver parameters
  subsection Stokes solver parameters
    set Use direct solver for Stokes system = true
  end
end

subsection Material model
  set Model name = adjoint material model
  set Material averaging = none

  subsection Adjoint material model
    set Reference density = 1
    set Reference specific heat = 1
    set Reference temperature = 0
    set Thermal expansion coefficient = 0.5
    set Specific heat temperature coefficient = 0
    set Density differential for compositional field 1 = 0
    set Viscosity = 0.02
    set Viscosity temperature coefficient = 0
    set Viscosity composition coefficient = 2
    set Thermal conductivity = 1e-6
  end
end

subsection Adjoint

  subsection Temperature field
    subsection Target function
      set Variable names = x,y
      set Function expression = 0.42 + 0.11*x + 0.07*y + 0.03*sin(pi*x)
    end
  end

  subsection Diagnostics
    set Run direct Stokes viscosity derivative test = true
    set Direct Stokes viscosity derivative test epsilon = 1e-7
  end
end

========================================================================================
// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_temperature_eta_c_stokes_no_av.prm
========================================================================================

========================================================================================
// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_temperature_eta_t_stokes_no_av.prm
========================================================================================

# Eta-only temperature-control benchmark for T -> eta(T) -> Stokes -> T objective.
# Temperature does not affect density or cp; a fixed compositional density
# contrast provides the background flow whose viscosity sensitivity is tested.

set Additional shared libraries = $ASPECT_SOURCE_DIR/benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/plugin/libadjoint_material_model.so

include $ASPECT_SOURCE_DIR/benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature_composition_coupled_no_av.prm

set Output directory = output-adjoint-temperature-eta-t-stokes-no-av

subsection Solver parameters
  subsection Stokes solver parameters
    set Use direct solver for Stokes system = true
  end
end

subsection Initial temperature model
  subsection Function
    set Function constants = eps=0
    set Variable names = x,y
    set Function expression = 0.5 + 0.15 * sin(pi*x) * sin(pi*y) + eps * sin(2*pi*x) * sin(pi*y)
  end
end

subsection Initial composition model
  subsection Function
    set Function constants = asym=1e-3
    set Variable names = x,y
    set Function expression = 0.35 + 0.2 * cos(pi*x) * sin(pi*y) + asym * x * y * (1-y)
  end
end

subsection Adjoint

  subsection Temperature field
    subsection Target function
      set Variable names = x,y
      set Function expression = 0.42 + 0.11*x + 0.07*y + 0.03*sin(pi*x)
    end
  end

  subsection Diagnostics
    set Run direct Stokes viscosity derivative test = true
    set Direct Stokes viscosity derivative test epsilon = 1e-7
  end
end

subsection Material model
  set Model name = adjoint material model
  set Material averaging = none

  subsection Adjoint material model
    set Reference density = 1
    set Reference specific heat = 1
    set Reference temperature = 0
    set Thermal expansion coefficient = 0
    set Specific heat temperature coefficient = 0
    set Density differential for compositional field 1 = 0.3
    set Viscosity = 0.02
    set Viscosity temperature coefficient = 2
    set Viscosity composition coefficient = 0
    set Thermal conductivity = 1e-6
  end
end

========================================================================================
// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/adjoint_temperature_eta_t_stokes_no_av.prm
========================================================================================
