========================================================================================
// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/run_taylor_test.py
========================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3

import math
import pathlib
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import argparse
from typing import Optional


ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
OBJECTIVE_RE = re.compile(r"Time-dependent (?:temperature|terminal) objective '[^']+' = ([0-9.eE+-]+)")


def read_prm_with_local_includes(path: pathlib.Path) -> str:
    text = path.read_text()
    expanded_lines = []
    for line in text.splitlines():
        stripped = line.strip()
        if stripped.startswith("include "):
            include_target = stripped.split(None, 1)[1]
            include_target = include_target.replace("$ASPECT_SOURCE_DIR", str(ROOT))
            include_path = pathlib.Path(include_target)
            if not include_path.is_absolute():
                include_path = path.parent / include_path
            expanded_lines.append(read_prm_with_local_includes(include_path))
        else:
            expanded_lines.append(line)
    return "\n".join(expanded_lines) + "\n"


def write_prm(base_prm: pathlib.Path,
              run_dir: pathlib.Path,
              epsilon: float,
              end_step: int,
              constant_name: str,
              trajectory_storage_mode: Optional[str]) -> pathlib.Path:
    prm_text = read_prm_with_local_includes(base_prm)
    prm_text = prm_text.replace(f"{constant_name}=0",
                                f"{constant_name}={epsilon:.16e}")
    prm_text = re.sub(r"set End step\s*=.*",
                      f"set End step = {end_step}",
                      prm_text,
                      count=1)
    prm_text = re.sub(r"set Output directory\s*=.*",
                      f"set Output directory = {run_dir / 'output'}",
                      prm_text)
    if trajectory_storage_mode is not None:
        # Appending a second Adjoint subsection intentionally overrides the
        # base benchmark without changing its physics or included files.
        prm_text += ("\nsubsection Adjoint\n"
                     f"  set Trajectory storage mode = {trajectory_storage_mode}\n"
                     "end\n")
    prm_path = run_dir / "input.prm"
    prm_path.write_text(prm_text)
    return prm_path


def run_model(binary: pathlib.Path,
              base_prm: pathlib.Path,
              work_dir: pathlib.Path,
              epsilon: float,
              end_step: int,
              constant_name: str,
              trajectory_storage_mode: Optional[str]):
    run_dir = work_dir / f"eps_{epsilon:.1e}".replace("+", "")
    run_dir.mkdir()
    prm_path = write_prm(base_prm, run_dir, epsilon, end_step, constant_name,
                         trajectory_storage_mode)
    completed = subprocess.run([str(binary), str(prm_path)],
                               cwd=ROOT,
                               text=True,
                               stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                               stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                               check=False)
    (run_dir / "screen-output").write_text(completed.stdout)
    if completed.returncode != 0:
        print(completed.stdout)
        raise RuntimeError(f"ASPECT failed for epsilon={epsilon} with exit code {completed.returncode}")

    match = OBJECTIVE_RE.search(completed.stdout)
    if match is None:
        print(completed.stdout)
        raise RuntimeError(f"Could not find objective value for epsilon={epsilon}")

    return float(match.group(1)), run_dir / "output"


def perturbation_value(x: float, y: float) -> float:
    return math.sin(2.0 * math.pi * x) * math.sin(math.pi * y)


def adjoint_directional_derivative(output_dir: pathlib.Path, gradient_field: str) -> float:
    if gradient_field == "temperature":
        gradient_file = output_dir / "initial_temperature_gradient_rank_00000.txt"
    elif gradient_field.startswith("composition:"):
        composition_name = gradient_field.split(":", 1)[1]
        gradient_file = output_dir / f"initial_composition_gradient_{composition_name}_rank_00000.txt"
    else:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Unsupported gradient field: {gradient_field}")

    total = 0.0
    with gradient_file.open() as handle:
        for line in handle:
            if line.startswith("#") or not line.strip():
                continue
            fields = line.split()
            gradient = float(fields[1])
            x = float(fields[2])
            y = float(fields[3])
            total += gradient * perturbation_value(x, y)
    return total


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run a finite-difference Taylor test for the time-dependent temperature adjoint benchmark.")
    parser.add_argument("binary", nargs="?", default=str(ROOT / "build-debug" / "aspect-debug"),
                        help="Path to the ASPECT executable.")
    parser.add_argument("--keep", action="store_true",
                        help="Keep the temporary run directories for inspection.")
    parser.add_argument("--end-step", type=int, default=1,
                        help="Termination end step used for every ASPECT run.")
    parser.add_argument("--base-prm", default=str(ROOT / "benchmarks" / "adjoint_time_dependent_temperature" / "adjoint_time_dependent_temperature.prm"),
                        help="Base parameter file to perturb.")
    parser.add_argument("--epsilons", default="1e-2,5e-3,2.5e-3,1.25e-3,6.25e-4",
                        help="Comma-separated perturbation amplitudes.")
    parser.add_argument("--tolerance", type=float, default=5.0e-2,
                        help="Maximum allowed relative slope error on the finest epsilon.")
    parser.add_argument("--absolute-slope-tolerance", type=float, default=1.0e-12,
                        help="Treat slopes below this absolute scale as zero when deciding pass/fail.")
    parser.add_argument("--constant-name", default="eps",
                        help="Function constant that is replaced by the perturbation amplitude.")
    parser.add_argument("--gradient-field", default="temperature",
                        help="Gradient file to test: 'temperature' or 'composition:<field_name>'.")
    parser.add_argument("--trajectory-storage-mode",
                        choices=("minimal", "stokes cached"),
                        help="Override the archive mode in every temporary input.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    binary = pathlib.Path(args.binary)
    if not binary.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(f"ASPECT binary does not exist: {binary}")
    base_prm = pathlib.Path(args.base_prm)
    if not base_prm.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(f"Base parameter file does not exist: {base_prm}")

    tmp_root = pathlib.Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="aspect-adjoint-taylor-"))
    try:
        j0, output0 = run_model(binary, base_prm, tmp_root, 0.0, args.end_step,
                                 args.constant_name, args.trajectory_storage_mode)
        prediction = adjoint_directional_derivative(output0, args.gradient_field)

        epsilons = [float(value) for value in args.epsilons.split(",") if value.strip()]
        if len(epsilons) == 0:
            raise RuntimeError("At least one epsilon is required.")

        errors = []
        remainders = []
        previous_epsilon = None
        print(f"end step = {args.end_step}")
        print(f"base prm = {base_prm}")
        print(f"gradient field = {args.gradient_field}")
        print(f"trajectory storage mode = {args.trajectory_storage_mode or 'base input'}")
        print(f"J(0) = {j0:.16e}")
        print(f"adjoint directional derivative = {prediction:.16e}")
        print("# epsilon finite_difference relative_slope_error taylor_remainder observed_order")
        for epsilon in epsilons:
            jplus, _ = run_model(binary, base_prm, tmp_root, epsilon, args.end_step,
                                  args.constant_name, args.trajectory_storage_mode)
            jminus, _ = run_model(binary, base_prm, tmp_root, -epsilon, args.end_step,
                                   args.constant_name, args.trajectory_storage_mode)
            finite_difference = (jplus - jminus) / (2.0 * epsilon)
            slope_error = (
                abs(finite_difference - prediction)
                / max(abs(finite_difference), abs(prediction), 1.0e-30)
            )
            remainder = max(abs(jplus - j0 - epsilon * prediction),
                            abs(jminus - j0 + epsilon * prediction))
            errors.append(slope_error)
            remainders.append(remainder)
            observed_order = float("nan")
            if previous_epsilon is not None and remainder > 0.0 and remainders[-2] > 0.0:
                observed_order = math.log(remainders[-2] / remainder) / math.log(previous_epsilon / epsilon)
            print(f"{epsilon:.8e} {finite_difference:.16e} {slope_error:.16e} {remainder:.16e} {observed_order:.8e}")
            previous_epsilon = epsilon

        finest_scale = max(abs(finite_difference), abs(prediction))
        if errors[-1] > args.tolerance and finest_scale > args.absolute_slope_tolerance:
            print(f"FAIL: finest relative slope error {errors[-1]:.3e} exceeds {args.tolerance:.3e}")
            return 1

        print("PASS: finite-difference slope agrees with adjoint prediction on the finest epsilon.")
        return 0
    finally:
        if args.keep:
            print(f"Kept temporary runs in {tmp_root}")
        else:
            shutil.rmtree(tmp_root)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

========================================================================================
// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/run_taylor_test.py
========================================================================================

========================================================================================
// BEGIN FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/run_trajectory_scaling.py
========================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Run reproducible load/scaling measurements for the adjoint trajectory archive.

The runner changes only archive mode, accepted-step count, dimension, and
initial global refinement.  It retains the fixed-mesh benchmark's material
model, timestep cap, nonlinear scheme, and solver tolerance.  Each run gets a
separate output directory and its complete ASPECT screen output is retained.
"""

import argparse
import csv
import json
import pathlib
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Tuple


ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
DEFAULT_PRM = (ROOT / "benchmarks" / "adjoint_time_dependent_temperature"
               / "trajectory_archive_benchmark.prm")

ARCHIVE_RE = re.compile(
    r"Adjoint trajectory archive \[reverse complete\]: mode = (?P<mode>.+?), "
    r"records = (?P<records>\d+), total bytes = (?P<total>\d+), "
    r"mean bytes/record = (?P<mean>\d+) "
    r"\(metadata (?P<metadata>\d+), T (?P<temperature>\d+), "
    r"C (?P<composition>\d+), u (?P<velocity>\d+), p (?P<pressure>\d+)\)")
MEMORY_RE = re.compile(
    r"Adjoint trajectory memory \[reverse complete\]: .*?"
    r"metadata object bytes = (?P<metadata_memory>\d+), "
    r"current loaded record bytes = (?P<current_record_memory>\d+), "
    r"current recovered Stokes-state bytes = (?P<recovered_stokes_memory>\d+), "
    r"reverse working-cache bytes = (?P<cache_memory>\d+), "
    r"VmPeak = (?P<vm_peak>\d+) kB, VmRSS = (?P<vm_rss>\d+) kB, "
    r"Stokes recovery solves = (?P<recovery_solves>\d+)")
TIMER_RE = re.compile(
    r"^\|\s*(?P<section>.*?)\s*\|\s*\d+\s*\|\s*"
    r"(?P<seconds>[0-9.eE+-]+)s\s*\|", re.MULTILINE)
TOTAL_TIME_RE = re.compile(
    r"Total wallclock time elapsed since start\s*\|\s*([0-9.eE+-]+)s")
DOF_RE = re.compile(r"Number of degrees of freedom: (?P<total>[\d,]+) \((?P<blocks>[^)]+)\)")
# ASPECT's /proc-derived VmPeak/VmRSS diagnostics are unavailable on macOS.
# `/usr/bin/time -l` supplies the corresponding process-level measurements
# there. The values reported by the macOS implementation are byte counts.
EXTERNAL_MAX_RSS_RE = re.compile(
    r"^\s*(?P<bytes>\d+)\s+maximum resident set size$", re.MULTILINE)
EXTERNAL_PEAK_FOOTPRINT_RE = re.compile(
    r"^\s*(?P<bytes>\d+)\s+peak memory footprint$", re.MULTILINE)


def parse_int_list(values: str) -> List[int]:
    result = [int(value) for value in values.split(",") if value.strip()]
    if not result or any(value <= 0 for value in result):
        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("Expected one or more positive integers.")
    return result


def replace_entry(text: str, name: str, value: str) -> str:
    pattern = re.compile(rf"^\s*set\s+{re.escape(name)}\s*=.*$", re.MULTILINE)
    replacement = f"set {name} = {value}"
    text, count = pattern.subn(replacement, text, count=1)
    if count != 1:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Could not replace parameter '{name}'.")
    return text


def write_input(base_text: str,
                path: pathlib.Path,
                output_dir: pathlib.Path,
                mode: str,
                steps: int,
                dimension: int,
                refinement: int) -> None:
    # The base has dt_max=0.01. Preserve it and adjust end time together with
    # the explicit end-step criterion so each load has the intended record
    # count, without invoking a new CFL schedule during replay.
    text = replace_entry(base_text, "Dimension", str(dimension))
    text = replace_entry(text, "End time", f"{steps * 0.01:.16g}")
    text = replace_entry(text, "Output directory", str(output_dir))
    text = replace_entry(text, "Trajectory storage mode", mode)
    text = replace_entry(text, "Run trajectory Stokes recovery diagnostic", "false")
    text = replace_entry(text, "End step", str(steps))
    text = replace_entry(text, "Initial global refinement", str(refinement))
    if dimension == 3:
        # The 2-D source expressions are z-independent, but ParsedFunction
        # still requires all three coordinate variables to be declared.
        text = re.sub(r"set Variable names\s*=\s*x,y\s*$",
                      "set Variable names = x,y,z", text, flags=re.MULTILINE)
        text = replace_entry(text, "Tangential velocity boundary indicators",
                             "left, right, front, back, bottom, top")
    path.write_text(text)


def parse_gradient_norm(path: pathlib.Path) -> float:
    total = 0.0
    for line in path.read_text().splitlines():
        if not line or line.startswith("#"):
            continue
        total += float(line.split()[1]) ** 2
    return total ** 0.5


def read_gradient(path: pathlib.Path) -> Dict[int, float]:
    values: Dict[int, float] = {}
    for line in path.read_text().splitlines():
        if line and not line.startswith("#"):
            fields = line.split()
            values[int(fields[0])] = float(fields[1])
    return values


def relative_gradient_difference(first: pathlib.Path, second: pathlib.Path) -> float:
    first_values = read_gradient(first)
    second_values = read_gradient(second)
    if first_values.keys() != second_values.keys():
        raise RuntimeError("Gradient DoF sets differ between archive modes.")
    diff_sq = sum((first_values[index] - second_values[index]) ** 2
                  for index in first_values)
    ref_sq = sum(value ** 2 for value in second_values.values())
    return diff_sq ** 0.5 / max(ref_sq ** 0.5, 1.0e-300)


def last_match(pattern: re.Pattern, text: str) -> re.Match:
    matches = list(pattern.finditer(text))
    if not matches:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Could not find expected output matching {pattern.pattern!r}.")
    return matches[-1]


def timer_values(text: str) -> Dict[str, float]:
    return {match.group("section").strip(): float(match.group("seconds"))
            for match in TIMER_RE.finditer(text)}


def run_case(binary: pathlib.Path,
             input_path: pathlib.Path,
             run_dir: pathlib.Path,
             measure_rss: bool) -> Tuple[Dict[str, object], pathlib.Path]:
    command = [str(binary), str(input_path)]
    if measure_rss:
        command = ["/usr/bin/time", "-l", *command]
    completed = subprocess.run(command, cwd=ROOT,
                               text=True, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                               stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, check=False)
    (run_dir / "screen-output").write_text(completed.stdout)
    if completed.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"ASPECT failed for {input_path}; inspect {run_dir / 'screen-output'}")

    archive = last_match(ARCHIVE_RE, completed.stdout).groupdict()
    memory = last_match(MEMORY_RE, completed.stdout).groupdict()
    dofs = last_match(DOF_RE, completed.stdout).groupdict()
    timers = timer_values(completed.stdout)
    output_dir = pathlib.Path(re.search(
        r"^set Output directory = (.+)$", input_path.read_text(), re.MULTILINE).group(1))
    gradient = output_dir / "initial_temperature_gradient_rank_00000.txt"
    if not gradient.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(f"Missing temperature gradient {gradient}.")

    result: Dict[str, object] = {
        "records": int(archive["records"]),
        "archive_bytes": int(archive["total"]),
        "bytes_per_record": int(archive["mean"]),
        "metadata_bytes": int(archive["metadata"]),
        "temperature_bytes": int(archive["temperature"]),
        "composition_bytes": int(archive["composition"]),
        "velocity_bytes": int(archive["velocity"]),
        "pressure_bytes": int(archive["pressure"]),
        "metadata_object_bytes": int(memory["metadata_memory"]),
        "current_record_memory_bytes": int(memory["current_record_memory"]),
        "recovered_stokes_memory_bytes": int(memory["recovered_stokes_memory"]),
        "reverse_cache_memory_bytes": int(memory["cache_memory"]),
        "vm_peak_kb": int(memory["vm_peak"]),
        "vm_rss_kb": int(memory["vm_rss"]),
        "external_max_rss_bytes": (
            int(last_match(EXTERNAL_MAX_RSS_RE, completed.stdout).group("bytes"))
            if measure_rss else None),
        "external_peak_footprint_bytes": (
            int(last_match(EXTERNAL_PEAK_FOOTPRINT_RE, completed.stdout).group("bytes"))
            if measure_rss else None),
        "stokes_recovery_solves": int(memory["recovery_solves"]),
        "dofs": int(dofs["total"].replace(",", "")),
        "dof_blocks": dofs["blocks"],
        "gradient_l2_norm": parse_gradient_norm(gradient),
        "write_seconds": timers.get("Adjoint trajectory write", 0.0),
        "read_seconds": timers.get("Adjoint trajectory read", 0.0),
        "recovery_seconds": timers.get("Adjoint trajectory Stokes recovery", 0.0),
        "reconstruction_seconds": timers.get("Adjoint trajectory state reconstruction", 0.0),
        "reverse_seconds": timers.get("Adjoint trajectory reverse sweep", 0.0),
        "total_seconds": float(last_match(TOTAL_TIME_RE, completed.stdout).group(1)),
        "gradient_path": str(gradient),
    }
    return result, gradient


def write_results(results: List[Dict[str, object]], results_dir: pathlib.Path) -> None:
    json_path = results_dir / "summary.json"
    csv_path = results_dir / "summary.csv"
    json_path.write_text(json.dumps(results, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n")
    with csv_path.open("w", newline="") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=sorted(results[0].keys()))
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(results)


def benchmark_levels(args: argparse.Namespace) -> Iterable[Tuple[str, int, int, int]]:
    if args.spatial_refinements:
        for refinement in args.spatial_refinements:
            yield (f"S{refinement}", args.spatial_steps, refinement, args.dimension)
    else:
        for steps in args.temporal_steps:
            yield (f"T{steps}", steps, args.refinement, args.dimension)


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("binary", nargs="?", default=str(ROOT / "build-debug" / "aspect-debug"))
    parser.add_argument("--base-prm", default=str(DEFAULT_PRM))
    parser.add_argument("--results-dir", required=True,
                        help="New directory for inputs, logs, and summary.{json,csv}.")
    parser.add_argument("--temporal-steps", type=parse_int_list,
                        default=parse_int_list("20,100,250,500,1000"))
    parser.add_argument("--spatial-refinements", type=parse_int_list,
                        help="Run a spatial hierarchy instead of temporal levels.")
    parser.add_argument("--spatial-steps", type=int, default=20)
    parser.add_argument("--dimension", type=int, choices=(2, 3), default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--refinement", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--measure-rss", action="store_true",
                        help="Capture macOS process RSS and physical footprint with /usr/bin/time -l.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    binary = pathlib.Path(args.binary).resolve()
    base_prm = pathlib.Path(args.base_prm).resolve()
    results_dir = pathlib.Path(args.results_dir).resolve()
    if not binary.exists() or not base_prm.exists():
        raise RuntimeError("The ASPECT executable or base parameter file is missing.")
    if results_dir.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(f"Refusing to overwrite existing results directory {results_dir}.")
    results_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    base_text = base_prm.read_text()
    results: List[Dict[str, object]] = []

    for label, steps, refinement, dimension in benchmark_levels(args):
        gradients: Dict[str, pathlib.Path] = {}
        level_results: Dict[str, Dict[str, object]] = {}
        for mode in ("minimal", "stokes cached"):
            mode_label = mode.replace(" ", "_")
            run_dir = results_dir / f"{label}_{mode_label}"
            run_dir.mkdir()
            output_dir = run_dir / "output"
            input_path = run_dir / "input.prm"
            write_input(base_text, input_path, output_dir, mode, steps,
                        dimension, refinement)
            result, gradients[mode] = run_case(binary, input_path, run_dir,
                                                args.measure_rss)
            result.update({"label": label, "mode": mode, "steps": steps,
                           "dimension": dimension, "initial_global_refinement": refinement,
                           "mpi_ranks": 1})
            level_results[mode] = result
            results.append(result)

        minimal = level_results["minimal"]
        cached = level_results["stokes cached"]
        gradient_difference = relative_gradient_difference(gradients["minimal"],
                                                            gradients["stokes cached"])
        for result in (minimal, cached):
            result["minimal_vs_cached_gradient_relative_l2"] = gradient_difference
        minimal["storage_saving"] = 1.0 - minimal["archive_bytes"] / cached["archive_bytes"]
        minimal["reverse_time_penalty"] = ((minimal["reverse_seconds"] - cached["reverse_seconds"])
                                            / max(cached["reverse_seconds"], 1.0e-300))
        minimal["stokes_recovery_fraction"] = (minimal["recovery_seconds"]
                                                / max(minimal["reverse_seconds"], 1.0e-300))
        for result in (minimal, cached):
            result["io_fraction"] = ((result["read_seconds"] + result["write_seconds"])
                                      / max(result["total_seconds"], 1.0e-300))
            result["write_bandwidth_bytes_per_second"] = (result["archive_bytes"]
                                                            / max(result["write_seconds"], 1.0e-300))
            result["read_bandwidth_bytes_per_second"] = (result["archive_bytes"]
                                                           / max(result["read_seconds"], 1.0e-300))

        print(f"{label}: records={minimal['records']}; gradient rel-L2={gradient_difference:.3e}; "
              f"minimal/cached bytes={minimal['archive_bytes']}/{cached['archive_bytes']}; "
              f"minimal/cached reverse s={minimal['reverse_seconds']:.6g}/{cached['reverse_seconds']:.6g}")

    write_results(results, results_dir)
    print(f"Wrote {results_dir / 'summary.csv'} and {results_dir / 'summary.json'}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

// END FILE: benchmarks/adjoint_time_dependent_temperature/run_trajectory_scaling.py

# Shared MPI trajectory backend: validation record

This record covers the fixed-mesh, same-rank-count, same-DoF-ownership
trajectory contract. It was run on 2026-09-07 with the Debug MPI executable
`build-debug/aspect-debug`, two MPI ranks where stated, and the repository's
1 GiB RSS guard. It is a correctness and local-filesystem characterization,
not a distributed-filesystem performance claim.

## Backend contract

Each rank serializes only its local payload. `MPI_Exscan` computes the local
`uint64_t` prefix; `LargeCount::File_write_at_all_c` writes directly to that
range in one `trajectory_XXXXXX.bin` segment. Payload synchronization and close
complete before rank zero atomically replaces `manifest`. The manifest is the
only authority for replay. It holds the record step/time/dt, mode, schema,
segment and record range, MPI rank count, every rank's range and byte count,
field-byte metadata, and ownership fingerprint. No trajectory values are
gathered to rank zero.

The tested layout is intentionally not repartition-capable. It requires the
same rank count, fixed mesh, global DoF numbering, and owned-index partition.

## S0--S6 correctness and lifecycle

| Stage | Fixture and observation | Result |
|---|---|---|
| S0 | Serial shared-file smoke/reverse | PASS |
| S1 | Fresh two-rank M2 run: 21 records, six data segments, 77,448 data bytes and a 3,260-byte manifest | PASS |
| S2 | Gradient L2 `0.013480703099710863`; existing validated M2 gradient files byte-identical; supplied scalar baseline differs only by `~1.5e-16` relative rounding | PASS |
| S3 | Three-state replay loaded records 8, 7, 6 across segments 2 and 1 | PASS |
| S4 | M2 stop at the interior reverse checkpoint, then a new `mpirun -n 2` resume | PASS; cold restart and final gradient were byte-identical to fresh M2 |
| S5 | M4 safe-incremental stop/resume | PASS; at stop the manifest retained records 0--9 in three segments; only fully obsolete segments were deleted |
| S6 | Controlled append failures at local-serialization, collective-write, durable-before-manifest, and post-manifest points | PASS; no deadlock and no RSS-guard termination |

For S6, injection at record 6 yielded these durable frontiers:

| Injection point | Manifest records / last | Segment 1 bytes | Interpretation |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| After local serialization | 6 / 5 | 7,376 | No record-6 shared write occurred. |
| After collective write, before manifest | 6 / 5 | 11,064 | Record-6 bytes are trailing and unlisted. |
| After payload durability, before manifest | 6 / 5 | 11,064 | Record-6 bytes are trailing and unlisted. |
| After manifest publication | 7 / 6 | 11,064 | Record 6 is committed and visible. |

`open_existing_archive()` reads only manifest entries and `load()` uses the
manifest-published local offset and size. The successful M2 restart exercises
that reopen path; trailing bytes from the two pre-manifest append cases are not
named by any logical record and are consequently unreachable.

## S7 file-count characterization

All rows use 2 ranks, 1000 requested steps (1001 records including time zero),
`stokes cached`, `Trajectory cleanup policy = keep`, and the same 268-DoF
fixture. Archive bytes include the manifest. Timings are one local Debug run.

| Records/segment | Shared data files | Metadata files | Archive bytes | Write | Reverse / read |
|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 1 | 1001 | 1 | 3,841,711 | 3.12 s | 5.81 s / 0.760 s |
| 4 | 251 | 1 | 3,843,880 | 2.40 s | 4.99 s / 0.628 s |
| 16 | 63 | 1 | 3,844,286 | 2.22 s | 4.81 s / 0.606 s |
| 64 | 16 | 1 | 3,844,641 | 2.25 s | 5.07 s / 0.642 s |
| 100 | 11 | 1 | 3,844,481 | 3.89 s | 11.5 s / 1.78 s |

The raw data payload was 3,691,688 bytes in every row. Data-file count is
`ceil(records / records_per_segment)` and does not depend on rank count. Thus
5000 steps with 100 records per segment needs about 50 shared data files (plus
the manifest), even at 1024 ranks.

## Reproduction inputs

- `adjoint_shared_trajectory_serial.prm`
- `adjoint_mpi_m2_baseline.prm`, `adjoint_mpi_m2_stop.prm`, and
  `adjoint_mpi_m2_resume.prm`
- `adjoint_mpi_m4_stop.prm` and `adjoint_mpi_m4_resume.prm`
- `adjoint_mpi_m2_shared_append_*.prm`
- `adjoint_mpi_m2_shared_scaling_{1,4,16,64,100}.prm`

Run MPI fixtures through `run_adjoint_mpi_m2_guard.zsh`; it samples process RSS,
captures diagnostics at 500 MiB, and terminates at 1 GiB. `git diff --check`
and `cmake --build build-debug --target aspect.exe.debug -j 2` passed after
the validation set.

# Adjoint trajectory archive

The time-dependent adjoint stores its forward trajectory in
`<Output directory>/adjoint_trajectory/`. This is a fixed-mesh replay archive,
not an ASPECT restart checkpoint. MPI restart assumes the forward and reverse
sweeps use the same rank count, mesh, DoF numbering, and DoF ownership
partition. It deliberately does not support repartitioning or a different rank
count.

The physical backend is a shared MPI-IO archive: a root-published `manifest`
and finite `trajectory_000000.bin`, `trajectory_000001.bin`, … segments. A
logical accepted step remains the public unit of `store`, `load`, and cleanup.
Each rank serializes only its own payload; an `MPI_Exscan` byte prefix selects
its range, and `MPI_File_write_at_all` writes that range directly to the shared
segment. No trajectory values are gathered to rank zero. The manifest records
schema, storage mode, timestep metadata, segment/range, rank count, rank-local
ranges and sizes, and ownership fingerprints. It is the only replay authority:
bytes from a failed append are unlisted and never valid records.

## Dependency audit

| Reverse dependency | Source | Archive treatment |
|---|---|---|
| Current temperature | `solution`, `current_linearization_point` | Stored once in record `n` |
| Current compositions | `solution`, `current_linearization_point` | Stored once in record `n` |
| Current velocity/pressure | Stokes and advection assembly | Recovered from `T,C` in `minimal`; stored once in `stokes cached` |
| `q_(n-1)`, `q_(n-2)` fields and velocities | BDF2, extrapolated velocity, entropy viscosity | Loaded by record index into a three-state reverse cache |
| `time`, `dt`, prior `dt` | BDF coefficients and time-dependent BCs | Global metadata table; prior `dt` is `metadata[n-1]` |
| Current constraints | Stokes/advection assembly | Rebuilt from fixed mesh, time, and boundary managers; never archived |
| Stokes/advection linearization points | Picard assembly | Rebuilt from indexed states under the v1 `rho(T,C)`, `cp(T)`, `eta(T,C)` restrictions |
| Material outputs and entropy-viscosity data | Assembly/EV replay | Recomputed |
| Matrices, preconditioners, solver state | Linear solves | Rebuilt; never archived |

The supported configuration rejects scheduled or periodic mesh refinement.
Initial refinement before the forward trajectory is allowed because all stored
records share the resulting fixed mesh.

## Modes

```text
subsection Adjoint
  set Trajectory storage mode = minimal
end
```

`minimal` stores `T,C` only. `stokes cached` also stores `u,p`:

```text
subsection Adjoint
  set Trajectory storage mode = stokes cached
  set Run trajectory Stokes recovery diagnostic = true
end
```

The optional diagnostic samples the first, middle, and final records, recovers
Stokes from archived `T,C`, and compares velocity and gauge-normalized pressure
to the cached forward result. The tolerance is configured by
`Trajectory Stokes recovery tolerance`.

## Shared-file layout

`Trajectory records per segment` controls the finite shared-file grouping and
defaults to `4`:

```text
subsection Adjoint
  set Trajectory records per segment = 4
end
```

The value is part of the manifest contract and must be unchanged when a
reverse restart reopens the archive. It changes file count, not logical replay:
the reverse algorithm continues to address records only by `store(step)`,
`load(step)`, `has_record(step)`, and `reclaim(...)`. Each record contains a
contiguous sequence of rank-local byte ranges. The manifest names the segment,
record offset and size, and every rank's offset, size, ownership fingerprint,
and field-byte breakdown.

## Retention and cleanup policy (v1)

The v1 policy is **explicit retention, followed by explicit cleanup**. The
archive is created at the first accepted forward state in
`<Output directory>/adjoint_trajectory/`; it must remain intact through the
whole reverse sweep and any optional recovery diagnostic.

Archive lifetime is selected with `Trajectory cleanup policy`. `safe
incremental` deletes only records made obsolete by a fully published reverse
checkpoint. At `next_reverse_step = n`, records `[n,last]` are obsolete while
the next operation still needs `n-1,n-2,n-3`. Logical unavailability is first
published in the manifest. Rank zero then removes a shared segment only when
every record in it is unavailable; segments are never compacted. An interrupted
cleanup can therefore leave an extra reclaimable segment, but cannot publish a
record that names deleted data. `on success` retains every record
through the reverse sweep and deletes the archive collectively after the final
gradient is written; `keep` retains it after success. No policy deletes archive
data on an exception or manager destruction. The archive is not a cross-layout
replay artifact: a later process may use it only with the same MPI partitioning
and DoF numbering.

Use a fresh output directory for every ASPECT launch. Before reusing an output
directory, first verify that the preceding run has exited and that its
gradients, screen output, and any failure evidence have been retained as
needed. Then remove **only** that run's exact
`<Output directory>/adjoint_trajectory/` subdirectory. Do not remove the
parent output directory or use a broad wildcard. This prevents a later run
from confusing stale shared segments or a stale manifest with its own archive.

For failed or interrupted runs, retain the archive until diagnosis is complete;
then apply the same exact-subdirectory cleanup if desired. Safe-incremental
recovery repeats its committed deletion frontier before resuming the reverse
sweep, so a crash during deletion is safe to restart. A partially written
`adjoint_restart/checkpoint_*.tmp` directory is never a valid restart target;
only the published `adjoint_restart/latest` generation is selected.

## Measurements

The simulator reports per-mode field-byte totals, mean bytes per record,
metadata/cache object memory, and `VmPeak`/`VmRSS` at archive completion and
after the reverse sweep. MPI safe-incremental cleanup additionally emits one
`ASPECT_ADJOINT_MPI_CLEANUP` line per rank with the reverse frontier, retained
record count, rank-local deleted records/files, and reclaimed bytes. The fixed
timer section names are:

```text
Adjoint trajectory write
Adjoint trajectory read
Adjoint trajectory Stokes recovery
Adjoint trajectory state reconstruction
Adjoint trajectory reverse sweep
```

Enable ASPECT's `timing statistics` and `memory statistics` postprocessors in
the benchmark input to retain the global timer and standard object-memory
statistics alongside these archive-specific reports. Compare the two modes on
the same forward trajectory; no sparse checkpointing, recomputation windows,
Revolve, AMR replay, lossy compression, or cross-job replay is included here.

The shared-MPI backend's serial/MPI, lifecycle, append-failure, and 1001-record
file-count results are retained in
[`shared_mpi_trajectory_validation.md`](shared_mpi_trajectory_validation.md).

# Fixed-mesh, multi-step regression/measurement case for the disk-backed
# time-dependent temperature-adjoint trajectory archive.  It deliberately
# uses the simple material model, so Stokes recovery depends only on T and C.

set Dimension                              = 2
set CFL number                             = 1
set End time                               = 0.20
set Output directory                       = output-adjoint-trajectory-archive-cached
set Pressure normalization                 = surface
set Surface pressure                       = 0
set Use years instead of seconds           = false
set Nonlinear solver scheme                = single Advection, single Stokes, adjoint temperature
set Maximum time step                      = 0.01

subsection Adjoint
  set List of objectives = temperature field

  subsection Temperature field
    set Coordinate system = cartesian

    subsection Target function
      set Variable names = x,y
      set Function expression = 0.45 + 0.05 * sin(2*pi*x) * sin(pi*y)
    end

    subsection Weight function
      set Variable names = x,y
      set Function expression = 1
    end
  end

  subsection Time dependent
    set Trajectory storage mode = stokes cached
  end

  subsection Diagnostics
    set Run trajectory Stokes recovery diagnostic = true
    set Trajectory Stokes recovery tolerance = 1e-8
  end
end

subsection Termination criteria
  set Termination criteria = end step
  set End step = 20
end

subsection Formulation
  set Formulation = Boussinesq approximation
end

subsection Geometry model
  set Model name = box

  subsection Box
    set X extent = 1
    set Y extent = 1
    set Z extent = 1
  end
end

subsection Initial temperature model
  set Model name = function

  subsection Function
    set Function constants = eps=0
    set Variable names = x,y
    set Function expression = 0.5 + 0.1 * sin(pi*x) * sin(pi*y) + eps * sin(2*pi*x) * sin(pi*y)
  end
end

subsection Boundary temperature model
  set List of model names = box
  set Fixed temperature boundary indicators = bottom, top

  subsection Box
    set Bottom temperature = 0.5
    set Top temperature    = 0.5
  end
end

subsection Boundary velocity model
  set Tangential velocity boundary indicators = left, right, bottom, top
end

subsection Gravity model
  set Model name = vertical

  subsection Vertical
    set Magnitude = 1
  end
end

subsection Material model
  set Model name = simple

  subsection Simple model
    set Reference density             = 1
    set Reference specific heat       = 1
    set Reference temperature         = 0
    set Thermal conductivity          = 1e-6
    set Thermal expansion coefficient = 1e-3
    set Viscosity                     = 1
  end
end

subsection Mesh refinement
  set Initial adaptive refinement        = 0
  set Initial global refinement          = 2
  set Time steps between mesh refinement = 0
end

subsection Checkpointing
  set Time between checkpoint  = 0
  set Steps between checkpoint = 0
end

# The recovery check compares independently solved Stokes states at 1e-8.
# Solve the forward and replay systems appreciably more accurately than that.
subsection Solver parameters
  subsection Stokes solver parameters
    set Linear solver tolerance = 1e-12
  end
end

subsection Postprocess
  set List of postprocessors = timing statistics, memory statistics
end

# Adjoint Trajectory Archive v1: Progressive Stress Results

## Scope

This is a measurement-only campaign. It uses the existing 3-D fixed-box,
single-rank, production-GMG benchmark with the same simple material model,
temperature objective, zero compositional fields, tolerances (`1e-12` Stokes
linear tolerance), and archive implementation in both modes. Measurements use
the ASPECT debug executable, so absolute timings are a local development limit
rather than release-build throughput. No archive, solver, or adjoint-physics
change was made for these runs.

`/usr/bin/time -l` supplies process-level memory because the ASPECT/deal.II
`VmPeak` and `VmRSS` fields are `0` on this macOS host. The host has 16 GiB of
physical memory; the safety threshold was therefore 11.2--12.0 GiB. All cases
use one MPI rank, so the process maximum is both the maximum and mean across
ranks. Archive sizes below are decimal MB; MiB/GiB are stated explicitly.

The raw per-case JSON and CSV contain all runner fields, including the archive
object, current-record, recovered-state, and three-state-cache byte counts:

- `/private/tmp/adjoint-trajectory-stress-r2-t50-20260905/summary.json`
- `/private/tmp/adjoint-trajectory-stress-r3-t50-20260905/summary.json`
- `/private/tmp/adjoint-trajectory-stress-r4-t50-20260905/summary.json`
- `/private/tmp/adjoint-trajectory-stress-r5-q5-20260905/summary.json`
- `/private/tmp/adjoint-trajectory-stress-r3-t250-20260905/summary.json`

For every comparison,
`E_g = ||g_min - g_cached||_2 / max(||g_cached||_2, 1e-50)`.
The denominator is much larger than `1e-50` in every reported case.

## Spatial scaling

The geometric fixed-mesh hierarchy produces 3,041, 20,381, 148,661, and
1,134,437 total DoFs rather than each nominal target in the plan. Block order
is velocity / pressure / temperature / composition; composition is zero in
every case. `S5-Q5` is the mandatory 5-step safety qualification, while the
first three levels completed the normal 50-step paired measurement.

| Level | Total DoFs (u / p / T / C) | Steps / records | Mode | MB/record | Archive MB | Recovery s/record | Reverse s/record | OS peak RSS GiB | `E_g` |
|---|---:|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| S2 | 3,041 (2,187 / 125 / 729 / 0) | 50 / 51 | minimal | 0.00587 | 0.299 | 0.0355 | 0.1045 | 0.153 | 6.55e-15 |
| S2 | same | 50 / 51 | stokes cached | 0.02438 | 1.243 | 0 | 0.0718 | 0.153 | 6.55e-15 |
| S3 | 20,381 (14,739 / 729 / 4,913 / 0) | 50 / 51 | minimal | 0.03934 | 2.006 | 0.2784 | 0.8275 | 0.198 | 6.87e-15 |
| S3 | same | 50 / 51 | stokes cached | 0.16310 | 8.318 | 0 | 0.5588 | 0.198 | 6.87e-15 |
| S4 | 148,661 (107,811 / 4,913 / 35,937 / 0) | 50 / 51 | minimal | 0.28753 | 14.664 | 2.1373 | 6.5098 | 0.524 | 5.28e-15 |
| S4 | same | 50 / 51 | stokes cached | 1.18934 | 60.656 | 0 | 4.7255 | 0.495 | 5.28e-15 |
| S5-Q5 | 1,134,437 (823,875 / 35,937 / 274,625 / 0) | 5 / 6 | minimal | 2.19703 | 13.182 | 18.6667 | 43.3333 | 1.728 | 1.68e-15 |
| S5-Q5 | same | 5 / 6 | stokes cached | 9.07554 | 54.453 | 0 | 33.0000 | 1.737 | 1.68e-15 |

Field-wise archive breakdown for the same cases (MB except metadata, which is
bytes):

| Level / mode | Temperature | Composition | Velocity | Pressure | Metadata |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| S2 minimal / cached | 0.298 / 0.298 | 0 / 0 | 0 / 0.893 | 0 / 0.051 | 1,381 |
| S3 minimal / cached | 2.005 / 2.005 | 0 / 0 | 0 / 6.014 | 0 / 0.298 | 1,381 |
| S4 minimal / cached | 14.663 / 14.663 | 0 / 0 | 0 / 43.987 | 0 / 2.005 | 1,381 |
| S5-Q5 minimal / cached | 13.182 / 13.182 | 0 / 0 | 0 / 39.546 | 0 / 1.725 | 136 |

The reverse cache is exactly three reconstructed full states: 0.112, 0.736,
5.354, and 40.842 MB at S2--S5. The corresponding current-record/recovered
Stokes-state sizes in minimal mode are 0.037/0.037, 0.245/0.245,
1.785/1.785, and 13.614/13.614 MB. Metadata objects are 4,295 bytes at the
50-step levels and 711 bytes for S5-Q5 (cached mode differs by 8 bytes).
Cached mode has no recovered Stokes-state allocation. These bounded values
show no cache growth with reverse progress.

At S4, minimal mode saved 75.82% of archive storage, with a 37.76% reverse
time penalty; recovery was 32.83% of minimal reverse time. At S5-Q5 the same
figures were 75.79%, 31.31%, and 43.08%. I/O was at most 0.15% of total time
in these two minimal cases and 0.12%/0.08% in cached mode, respectively.

## 3-D temporal scaling at fixed 20,381 DoFs

The 20-, 50-, 100-, and 250-step measurements use exactly the S3 mesh. The
100-step point is also the combined spatial-temporal case. Process RSS was
not captured for the earlier pre-wrapper 20- and 100-step runs; all ASPECT
internal Vm values were unavailable on this host, not zero-memory readings.

| Steps / records | Mode | Archive MB | MB/record | Write / read s | Recovery s | Reverse s | Recovery / reverse | OS peak RSS GiB | `E_g` |
|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 20 / 21 | minimal | 0.826 | 0.03934 | 0.0082 / 0.0032 | 5.72 | 16.2 | 35.31% | — | 2.97e-15 |
| 20 / 21 | stokes cached | 3.425 | 0.16310 | 0.0174 / 0.0089 | 0 | 11.2 | — | — | 2.97e-15 |
| 50 / 51 | minimal | 2.006 | 0.03934 | 0.0217 / 0.0084 | 14.2 | 42.2 | 33.65% | 0.198 | 6.87e-15 |
| 50 / 51 | stokes cached | 8.318 | 0.16310 | 0.0561 / 0.0235 | 0 | 28.5 | — | 0.198 | 6.87e-15 |
| 100 / 101 | minimal | 3.973 | 0.03934 | 0.0455 / 0.0165 | 27.8 | 83.9 | 33.13% | — | 8.34e-15 |
| 100 / 101 | stokes cached | 16.473 | 0.16310 | 0.0876 / 0.0447 | 0 | 55.7 | — | — | 8.34e-15 |
| 250 / 251 | minimal | 9.874 | 0.03934 | 0.1250 / 0.1590 | 67.5 | 208.0 | 32.45% | 0.297 | 2.32e-14 |
| 250 / 251 | stokes cached | 40.938 | 0.16310 | 0.2240 / 0.1170 | 0 | 140.0 | — | 0.315 | 2.32e-14 |

For 250 steps, the minimal/cached storage saving is 75.88%; the minimal
reverse-time penalty is 48.57%; and I/O is 0.095% / 0.150% of total elapsed
time for minimal / cached. Minimal mode performed exactly one recovery solve
per record (21, 51, 101, and 251); cached mode performed none. The three-state
cache stayed 0.736 MB for every temporal length, while archive metadata grew
linearly from 559 bytes (21 records) to 6,959 bytes (251 records).

## Combined stress case

The completed combined case is **20,381 DoFs x 100 accepted steps** (101
records). It is intentionally below the plan's aspirational 500k x 250 case:
the next fixed refinement is 1.13M DoFs, whose short paired qualification
already costs 13.9 minutes. The completed combined result is nevertheless a
meaningful simultaneous spatial-and-temporal load and exercises both archive
modes through a full forward/reverse cycle.

| Case | Mode | Archive MB | Reverse s | Recovery fraction | Peak memory | Gradient difference |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| S3 x 100 | minimal | 3.973 | 83.9 | 33.13% | not captured | 8.34e-15 |
| S3 x 100 | stokes cached | 16.473 | 55.7 | 0% | not captured | 8.34e-15 |

## Recovery diagnostic and solver health

The cached S4 50-step diagnostic sampled records 0, 25, and 50 and then
completed the ordinary reverse sweep successfully. With GMG and the configured
`1e-12` linear tolerance, its velocity errors were `3.445e-10`, `3.498e-10`,
and `3.580e-10`; gauge-normalized pressure errors were `1.044e-13` at all
three records. Each sampled recovery converged in `13+0` GMG iterations and
passed the `1e-8` diagnostic threshold. Standard ASPECT output does not expose
a scalar final residual here; convergence is controlled by the configured
linear tolerance. The diagnostic's `/usr/bin/time -l` report showed 0 swaps
and 570,753,024 bytes peak RSS.

This does not supersede the documented caveat for time-dependent inhomogeneous
tangential velocity: its small-case GMG replay discrepancy was approximately
`8.78e-5`, while direct recovery was exact. The stress benchmark has static
tangential velocity and supplies no contradictory evidence of missing archive
state.

## Scaling interpretation and practical limit

`S_record` tracks total DoFs very closely. Minimal bytes/record grow from
5,867 to 39,339 to 287,531 to 2,197,030 bytes as DoFs grow from 3,041 to
20,381 to 148,661 to 1,134,437. Cached bytes/record grow from 24,379 to
163,099 to 1,189,339 to 9,075,542. The near-matching refinement ratios support
`S_record = O(N_DoF)` for both modes. At fixed S3, archive sizes and write/
read/recovery times grow approximately linearly with records; bytes/record
remain stable to the reported precision. There is no evidence of hidden archive
growth, metadata accumulation beyond linear per-record metadata, cache leakage,
or nonlinear I/O degradation.

The largest completed spatial configuration is **S5-Q5: 1,134,437 DoFs x 5
steps**, with a maximum measured RSS of 1,865,285,632 bytes (1.74 GiB) and zero
swaps. It is memory-safe at only about 15% of the 11.2--12.0 GiB stop range,
but it is the practical local-development time limit for this debug campaign:
the paired Q5 run took 469 s + 364 s. Applying the observed S4 Q5-to-50
multiplier projects an S5 50-step pair at roughly 69 min minimal plus 57 min
cached, or about 2.1 hours. The largest normal 50-step spatial measurement is
therefore **S4: 148,661 DoFs x 50 steps**. This is a runtime stop, not an
archive correctness or memory failure.

## Storage estimates from the largest measured DoF level

These are estimates, not measurements. They assume the measured S5-Q5
bytes/record remain unchanged and include `steps + 1` stored records.

| Accepted timesteps | Records | Minimal archive | Stokes-cached archive |
|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 1,000 | 1,001 | 2.199 GB | 9.085 GB |
| 5,000 | 5,001 | 10.987 GB | 45.387 GB |
| 10,000 | 10,001 | 21.972 GB | 90.764 GB |

A purely linear, cautious extrapolation from S5 to 2,000,000 DoFs gives about
3.873 MB/record minimal and 16.000 MB/record cached. It is only a storage
estimate; it is not a predicted solver time or memory limit for an untested
global model.

## Archive retention and cleanup

V1 uses an explicit retention policy. The archive remains in
`<Output directory>/adjoint_trajectory/` after a successful reverse sweep and
also after a failed or interrupted run. This is intentional: it preserves
postmortem evidence, while providing no restart or cross-job-replay guarantee.
The workflow that owns the output directory must retain the files until the
gradient and logs have been accepted, then delete **only that exact archive
subdirectory** before the output directory is reused. Every new run should use
a fresh output directory. The detailed policy and stale-record rationale are
in [trajectory_archive.md](trajectory_archive.md#retention-and-cleanup-policy-v1).

## Result

The milestone's success conditions are met: four increasing spatial levels,
four increasing 3-D temporal levels, one completed combined case, paired-mode
comparisons, archive/timing/memory/gradient data, and a practical local limit
are all recorded. The explicit retention/cleanup policy closes the final v1
operational gap. The restricted fixed-mesh, same-process archive/restore v1 is
therefore fully closed out. The data support proceeding to the next planned
milestone: sparse trajectory checkpointing with deterministic forward-window
replay.

# Adjoint trajectory archive v1: close-out validation

This report records single-rank Debug measurements made on 2026-09-05 with
`build-debug/aspect-debug`. The archive is a fixed-mesh, lossless trajectory
format. All comparisons use the same physical input, timestep cap, and mesh
for the two storage modes. Timings are development-machine measurements, not
production performance claims.

The reproducible drivers are
[`run_taylor_test.py`](run_taylor_test.py) and
[`run_trajectory_scaling.py`](run_trajectory_scaling.py). The latter retains
per-run inputs and complete ASPECT screen output, and writes `summary.csv` and
`summary.json` to the user-selected results directory.

## Correctness

| Case | Minimal result | Stokes-cached result | Cross-mode result | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Constant-viscosity BDF2 Taylor (3 steps) | Taylor order `2.000`; finest slope error `1.99e-11` | Taylor order `2.000`; finest slope error `1.99e-11` | Gradient relative L2 `1.00e-12` | PASS |
| Iterative-Stokes recovery (21 records, linear tolerance `1e-12`) | 21 recovery solves | Cached comparison at records 0/10/20: velocity `< 8e-10`, gauge-normalized pressure `< 3e-14` | B0 gradient relative L2 `1.96e-15` | PASS |
| eta(T) direct Stokes recovery | n/a | Recovery error exactly zero; direct viscosity derivative relative error `3.3e-16` | n/a | PASS |
| eta(C) direct Stokes recovery | n/a | Recovery error exactly zero; viscosity-coefficient transpose relative error `4.15e-9` | n/a | PASS |
| Active entropy viscosity, 3 steps | Taylor order `1.99999967`; 4 recovery solves | Taylor order `1.99999967`; 0 recovery solves | Gradient relative L2 `1.18e-12` | PASS |
| Curved free slip, 3 steps | Taylor order `2.000`; 4 recovery solves | Taylor order `2.000`; 0 recovery solves | Gradient relative L2 `1.95e-12` | PASS |
| Time-dependent tangential velocity, 3 steps | Finest Taylor order `1.9963`; archived metadata `t={0,.01,.02}`, `dt={0,.01,.01}` | Direct-Stokes recovery is exactly zero at records 0/1/2 | Gradient relative L2 `5.94e-12` | PASS (see GMG note) |
| Composition objective, 4 records | 4 recovery solves | 0 recovery solves | Composition-gradient relative L2 `1.05e-12` | PASS |

The active-EV local entropy residual, global entropy denominator, and global
entropy variation diagnostics produced the same values in both modes (relative
errors `5.05e-7`, `1.98e-11`, and `1.70e-9`, respectively). This confirms that
the current/previous/previous-previous reconstructed cache path was exercised
without changing the validated EV algebra.

The known optional curved velocity-transpose diagnostic was not used as an
archive pass/fail criterion. The end-to-end Taylor test is second order in both
modes, and no archive-mode-specific discrepancy was observed.

For the time-dependent tangential-velocity input, a separate GMG recovery
diagnostic with `Linear solver tolerance = 1e-12` reported velocity relative
error `8.78e-5` at record zero (pressure error `1.41e-11`) and therefore did
not meet its independently requested `1e-8` velocity threshold. Repeating the
identical archived T,C/time/constraint reconstruction with ASPECT's direct
Stokes solver gives exactly zero velocity and pressure error at all three
records. The end-to-end minimal/cached Taylor and gradient comparisons above
remain mode-equivalent. This identifies an iterative inhomogeneous-constraint
solver reproducibility sensitivity, not missing archive state; it is retained
as a benchmark caveat rather than changing adjoint or archive physics.

## Temporal scaling (2D, 268 DoFs, one MPI rank)

Each row has `N+1` archived records because ASPECT writes the accepted state at
time zero and after every requested step. Byte counts include metadata.

| Requested steps / records | Mode | Archive bytes | Bytes/record | Write / read (s) | Recovery (s) / solves | Reverse (s) | Gradient difference |
|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 20 / 21 | minimal | 14,335 | 682 | .00338 / .000437 | .0788 / 21 | .176 | `1.96e-15` |
| 20 / 21 | stokes cached | 46,087 | 2,194 | .00905 / .00112 | 0 / 0 | .124 | `1.96e-15` |
| 100 / 101 | minimal | 69,031 | 683 | .0203 / .00250 | .387 / 101 | .926 | `4.44e-15` |
| 100 / 101 | stokes cached | 221,743 | 2,195 | .0306 / .00512 | 0 / 0 | .545 | `4.44e-15` |
| 250 / 251 | minimal | 171,615 | 683 | .0585 / .00571 | .962 / 251 | 2.33 | `9.01e-15` |
| 250 / 251 | stokes cached | 551,127 | 2,195 | .0831 / .0126 | 0 / 0 | 1.37 | `9.01e-15` |
| 500 / 501 | minimal | 342,583 | 683 | .165 / .0122 | 1.94 / 501 | 4.70 | `8.85e-15` |
| 500 / 501 | stokes cached | 1,100,095 | 2,195 | .210 / .0259 | 0 / 0 | 2.75 | `8.85e-15` |
| 1,000 / 1,001 | minimal | 684,531 | 683 | .454 / .0231 | 3.87 / 1,001 | 9.40 | `2.35e-14` |
| 1,000 / 1,001 | stokes cached | 2,198,043 | 2,195 | .548 / .0543 | 0 / 0 | 5.48 | `2.35e-14` |

The measured bytes per record are stable (`682–683` minimal and
`2,194–2,195` cached), verifying linear archive growth. At 1,001 records the
minimal storage saving is `68.86%`; its reverse-time penalty is `71.5%`, of
which Stokes recovery is `41.2%` of the minimal reverse sweep. Read/write I/O
was below `6.6%` of whole-run time on this local filesystem.

## Spatial scaling (3D, fixed 20 requested steps, one MPI rank)

The paired 3D S3 level has 20,381 DoFs: 14,739 velocity, 729 pressure, and
4,913 temperature. The field decomposition is reported directly by ASPECT.

| DoFs / records | Mode | Archive bytes / record | Total archive | Write / read (s) | Recovery / step (s) | Reverse / step (s) | Current / cache memory |
|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 20,381 / 21 | minimal | 39,338 | 826,111 | .00816 / .00320 | .272 | .771 | 245,340 / 736,020 B |
| 20,381 / 21 | stokes cached | 163,098 | 3,425,071 | .0174 / .00889 | 0 | .533 | 245,340 / 736,020 B |

The S3 gradient relative L2 difference is `2.97e-15`. Minimal storage saves
`75.88%`; its reverse penalty is `44.6%`, and Stokes recovery is `35.3%` of
its reverse time. The platform's deal.II memory-stat API returned `VmPeak =
VmRSS = 0 kB`, so those process-level numbers are explicitly unavailable here;
the object-level fields above remain valid.

An exploratory S4 minimal run reached 148,661 DoFs (107,811 velocity, 4,913
pressure, 35,937 temperature). Its 21-record archive was 6,038,143 B
(287,530 B/record), reverse time was 128 s, recovery time was 44 s, and the
three-record reverse cache was 5,354,100 B. It demonstrates a substantially
larger practical Debug load, but it is not included in a paired result because
the cached counterpart was deliberately not run after the 196 s minimal run.

## Combined stress benchmark

The S3 mesh with 100 requested steps (101 records) combines spatial and
temporal load without a Taylor sweep.

| DoFs / records | Mode | Archive bytes | Bytes/record | Write / read (s) | Recovery (s) / solves | Reverse (s) | Gradient relative L2 |
|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 20,381 / 101 | minimal | 3,973,287 | 39,339 | .0455 / .0165 | 27.8 / 101 | 83.9 | `8.34e-15` |
| 20,381 / 101 | stokes cached | 16,473,047 | 163,099 | .0876 / .0447 | 0 / 0 | 55.7 | `8.34e-15` |

For this combined case, minimal storage saves `75.88%`; the reverse-time
penalty is `50.6%`; Stokes recovery is `33.1%` of minimal reverse time; and
archive I/O is below `0.15%` of whole-run time.

## Close-out status

## Sparse replay Phase C/D qualification (2026-09-08)

The authoritative executable for this qualification was the non-unity MPI
Debug build, `build-no-unity-mpi/aspect-debug`. It rebuilt
`source/adjoint/core/manager.cc`; the trajectory-store and restart-checkpoint
headers were consequently compiled through that translation unit. The
accepted-step journal, anchor replay, and checkpoint recovery all use the
recorded timestep schedule and the ordinary simulator advance/start/solve path.

The 21-record, 268-DoF direct-Stokes control completed serial and two-rank
checks. K=5 restarts at `next_reverse_step=13` and `10` selected lower anchors
`10` and `5`, respectively; they replayed five transitions after restart and
produced byte-identical rank-local gradient files. Safe incremental cleanup
retained only the required lower anchor and reclaimed obsolete shared segments.
The serial and two-rank rank-local payload-deletion recovery fixture also
completed with byte-identical gradients. The serial logical global comparison
against the two-rank K=5 gradient was L2 `8.011591853955987e-13`, relative L2
`5.943007419354939e-11`, and max absolute `2.901637263796886e-13` over 82
global DoFs.

| Qualification | Result | Replay/reference result |
|---|---|---|
| Active entropy/artificial viscosity (`alpha=1`), direct Stokes | PASS | K=1/K=5 gradients byte-identical; state, velocity, and gauge-pressure differences 0 |
| Time-dependent tangential prescribed velocity, direct Stokes | PASS | K=1/K=5 gradients byte-identical; state, velocity, and gauge-pressure differences 0 |
| GMG, fixed mesh | PASS at configured `1e-10` replay tolerance | max state `1.87e-16`, velocity `2.91e-11`, pressure `3.05e-16`; K=1/K=5 gradient relative L2 `8.45e-16` |
| Active-EV K=5 restart at reverse step 5 | PASS | lower anchor 0, five restart-induced transitions, byte-identical gradient |

The physical reference archive must use `Trajectory storage mode = stokes
cached` whenever state/velocity/pressure replay validation is requested. A
minimal reference does not contain velocity or pressure and is not a valid
reference for those components.

### Anchor-spacing measurement

All rows below are fresh serial Debug runs on the same 20-step control case.
Archive bytes include the dense schedule and anchor payloads. Wall time is a
local development-machine measurement only; maximum resident set size was
about 151 MB in every run. Every sparse gradient was byte-identical to K=1.

|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|

The subsequent [progressive stress report](trajectory_archive_v1_stress.md)
supersedes this document's earlier 3-D scaling limit: it includes paired
148,661-DoF measurements, a 1.13M-DoF qualification, four 3-D temporal levels,
and the practical debug-runtime limit. The v1 lifecycle policy is now explicit:
retain `<Output directory>/adjoint_trajectory/` through reverse completion and
diagnosis, then have the owning workflow delete that exact subdirectory before
the output directory is reused. With that operational policy, the restricted
fixed-mesh, same-process v1 archive/restore scope is fully closed out.
